# Changelog

All notable changes to Castcut, one section per release. Generated from git history
(release boundaries are the repo's own `Release vX.Y.Z` commits, since not every tag is
mirrored to every clone). Full release notes with installer/image links are on
[GitHub Releases](https://github.com/doodersrage/castcut/releases).

Format loosely follows [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/).

## [Unreleased]

- **Preview the cut plays the narration.** The narration was only recorded at export, so you first heard it in the finished film. The Cut preview now plays each recorded line from its shot's start, with the music ducked under it. When lines aren't recorded yet, _Record narration to hear it (N lines)_ records them, and Cut film reuses those recordings (Day).
- **Fix: a couple beat's own clothes ended up on the partner.** "On his lap on the couch in a silk robe over a slip", with your outfit set, put the robe on him in 5 of 5 of your renders (3 re-rolled). When the outfit comes from the plate or Outfit, the beat's own clothes are now left out of the moment line. Same-seed replays: robe on him 3/3 → 0/3.
- **Fix: Turn around broke or half-turned in Firefox.** In Firefox the viewer could read no frames at all ("frames.bitmaps[0] is undefined") and flagged a full MiniMax-H3 turn as a part turn, so it played back and forth. Firefox can skip the frame callbacks for a video that isn't on the page, so the viewer now falls back to animation ticks and then to seeking, and shows a clear message if every way fails. A turn counts as full when any frame near the end matches the start, not only the last one (H3 ends a little short of the front). Firefox and Chromium now agree on every test clip.
- **Turn around and Runway clips on MiniMax-H3.** The turn is now made on MiniMax-H3 when ComfyUI has it, then WAN, then LTX-2.5 (it was always LTX-2.5). That's slower, a few minutes instead of about one, but the turn looks much better. The status line names the engine and how long to expect. Outfits that already have a runway clip keep theirs.
- **Look is retired.** The Look page (reference tiles → extract a look) is gone from Film, the dock, More, the sidebar and the phone tabs, and Cast home, Day and Outfit no longer link to it. A Cast's looks, look plates and Look notes stay on Cast home, and existing look packs still carry into Outfit, Day and Story. A film saved at Look resumes at Day, old `/moodboard` links open the Cast's looks, and phone Capture's _Start Look_ is now _Start Day_.
- **Look plates from behind, again.** Generate new plate on UltraReal still came out from behind (your look 2, twice); the lead now reads "full-length front-view … her face and the front of her body toward the camera", and the studio "backdrop" is a "studio wall" (same 8 seeds: back views 1 → 0, crops 2 → 1). A plate where no face is found was skipped silently by the likeness guard; it is now said as "no face — likely from behind" in the tray and on Cast home → Looks, with the earlier plate one click away.
- **Turn her around.** A _Turn around_ button on Outfit's try-on stage, and _Turn around_ on Wardrobe outfits that have a runway clip, open a viewer you drag (or ← →) to turn her around, spinning on its own until touched (not with reduced motion). Every angle is a real frame of the runway clip, so the face, fabric and shoes stay hers. The viewer keeps only the turn (LTX turns in ~2.5 s, then stands; found from frame-to-frame motion, since the background drifts too much to compare frames to the first) and wraps when the turn comes back round, else stops at the ends. Outfit makes the clip the first time (LTX, about a minute) and keeps it on the Wardrobe outfit once the try-on is kept. Frames are read by playing the clip once (~5 s); seeking frame by frame took ~30 s. Replaces the 3D-model attempt: TRELLIS.2 (even with the picture painted on) and Hunyuan3D 2.1 weren't presentable.
- **Fix: look plates from behind, topless or as a face close-up.** On a second Cast the full-length plate still came out side-on, from behind, topless ("plain light underwear" read as nude) or as a face close-up (her freckles were asked to be "clearly visible"): 0 of 4 usable. Plates now face the camera, wear a named base layer (a plain light-grey sports bra and briefs, both clearly on; boxer briefs for a man), and treat marks as small details of the full-length picture — clothed and facing 4 of 4, full length 3 of 4 on the same seeds.
- **Gallery: Needs a look, clip checks, Used in.** Each clip is checked as it lands: a hard cut inside it (MiniMax-H3 once cut a talking clip to a face close-up at 2.0 s) and, for an MP4, whether it has sound — on your clips the cut one read "cut at 2.1 s" and the continuous ones nothing. The checks filter is now _Needs a look_: a missed pose or face, a clip that cuts or came out silent, marks the repair couldn't put back (Day, Story and Outfit record them); the card badge says which, and _Redo these_ re-queues them on a new seed (24 at a time, in the Cast filter when one is picked). A still or clip that a cut film uses says so ("in a film", linking to it), and the lightbox's details list the films and what the checks found — so you know what's safe to delete. The pre-cut film check flags a clip that cuts too.
- **Fix: new look plates weren't full body.** Plates rendered on a square canvas, and UltraReal cut the figure at the thighs even when asked for "full-body". Plates now render on a 3:4 portrait canvas with a full-length opening line ("taken from a few metres away — the whole body from the top of the head to both feet and the floor under them"). On the same Cast and seeds that kept head to feet 3 of 3, against 1 of 2 for the portrait canvas alone and a thigh crop on the square one. Applies to Cast home's Generate plate, Look and Day's "Make a plate".
- **Fix: Outfit's Fix opened the full-size view instead of Fix an area.** It now goes straight to the fix dialog, and closing the dialog closes it all.
- **Fix: a new look plate was lost when you switched looks while it rendered.** Switching looks cleared the plate job, so the finished render attached nowhere. It now stays pinned to the look it was rendering for and lands there, whichever look is active. A job for a look you removed, or one still running when you put back an earlier plate, is dropped as before.
- **Look keeps the Cast the same person.** A new look plate — from Look, Cast home's Generate plate, Day's "Make a plate" or an upload — is now measured against the Cast as it lands: against the Default look's face (or, for the Default look, its previous plate). One that reads as someone else (below the drift line Cast home's _Compare the looks' plates_ uses) is said in the tray and on Cast home → Looks, with _Put back the earlier plate_. Then the Cast's marks are checked on the new plate, and missing glasses or tattoos are painted on once (Prepare plate → Her marks; the plate before stays in Earlier plates). The look also keeps the Look page's notes (mood, light, palette, place) when Look gives it its plate, and Story writes with the active look's notes — switch looks and the writer's light and palette switch too (your own Story hints stay). Look's plate preview, scene still and reference tile open in the lightbox.
- **Fix: MiniMax-H3 clips cutting to a face close-up.** A night talking clip on H3 cut from the wide shot to her face at 2.0 s. H3 has no negative prompt, and the camera sentence named what must not happen ("does not move, zoom or push in, and there is no close-up"). Replayed on its own seed it cut every time with that sentence and never with it said positively; H3 clips now get the camera as one fixed tripod framing, with no zoom / push-in / close-up words (LTX and WAN prompts are unchanged).
- **Fix: floating utensils.** "Dancing alone in the kitchen with a wooden spoon as a microphone" drew both arms up (the dance pose); the gesture check read the spoon as cooking, called the still a miss, and the redo asked her to be "visibly cooking with a utensil" — with the two-person dance cue ("the two people face each other…") on a solo still. Edit 2511 hung the spoons in the air. A utensil alone no longer counts as cooking, a redo's pose cue comes only from the gesture's own layout, and the beat now dances without a prop.
- **Outfit ties into Cast home.** _Keep_ puts the outfit straight into the Cast's Wardrobe (with the try-on as its picture) — before, an outfit got there only once a Day still was made in it. Try-ons now say the Cast's marks that the outfit shows and keep glasses out of "discard every accessory" (they were taking her glasses off), and once a try-on's front is final its marks are checked and, when missing, repaired in place (Day's check and Edit 2511 repair; the card keeps its outfit, dressed plate and back view). A partner Cast now wears their own kept outfit on Day's two-person stills — a clothing photo's description and their shoes too, not only a kit — and Day's partner row says what they wear, with _Dress {partner} in Outfit_. Wardrobe outfits get _Runway clip_: a short turn-around clip made from the outfit's picture (LTX-2.5), kept and played on the outfit.
- **Fix: email links pointed at localhost** ("Open gallery: http://127.0.0.1:47832/gallery"). The public address was only learned when an admin signed in, and a session that stays signed in never did. It is now also learned from a signed-in admin's page (its Referer, or the proxy's forwarded host); a localhost or LAN-IP visit never replaces a public name; and with none known yet, the first `PROMPT_TRUSTED_HOSTS` name (https) is used before localhost. `PROMPT_API_URL` still wins.
- **Cast home's Generate plate renders like Look.** It used whatever model the app was last left on (UltraReal only if Look had just set it); it now uses UltraReal Fine-Tune v4 when ComfyUI has it, otherwise Edit 2511.
- **Cast home pictures open full size.** The reference sheet's views and the Wardrobe's outfits open in the lightbox (the Wardrobe shows the full still, not its thumbnail); the arrow keys step through the sheet or the wardrobe.
- **Story catches up with Day.** _Cast details:_ a Story still now says the lead's marks that show (glasses, tattoos…) and the props the scene mentions, and a clothed scene the writer sets in her own kitchen, bedroom or office uses her home / office look from Cast home. The writer is told who the lead's people are (Sam is her sibling — family, never romantic), and a romantic or adult scene that names a family member is not drawn — its card says why. Solo Story stills get Day's marks check: a missing or misplaced mark gets one Edit 2511 repair pass (two at most), said on the scene card. _Story films:_ _Narrate the story_ reads each scene's first sentence over its shot (LTX-2.5 audio only; scenes with their own spoken line are left alone; a narrated still holds until its line ends — on Day too), _Widen stills to 16:9_ for a widescreen cut, _Previously on_ opens with three shots of the Cast's last Story, and _Add close-ups_ on a scene card cuts a face close-up and a detail in after it. _Story clips on → MiniMax-H3_ next to the clip engine note (image-to-video; text-to-video stays on the Video model).
- **Fewer open fridges in adult night scenes.** One of the two late-night adult Settings was "apartment at midnight lit by the open fridge", and a Setting goes into every still in its slot — so bed and couch scenes kept getting a glowing fridge behind them. That Setting is now lit by a single lamp, and the Raunchy oral beat at the fridge moved to the couch; the one beat that is about the fridge (solo, at the open fridge) keeps it.

## [v2.9.0] - 2026-10-14

- **Day scenes match their place.** A beat that named somewhere other than the slot's Setting (a gym beat in a bedroom, a beach beat at the office) mixed the two into one picture; the beat and the Setting now agree — Suggestive and sport/vacation days kept their beat's venue. On the clothed sweep, misfit Suggest-day stills went from about a third to none (24 of 24 live).
- **MiniMax-H3 for Day clips.** Video → _Day clips on_ now offers MiniMax-H3 next to LTX-2.5 and WAN: image-to-video with its own sound, the best motion and scene coherence of the three on the same four Day stills, lines spoken word for word with a fuller soundtrack than LTX — about 2½ minutes a clip (LTX ~30 s, WAN ~3 min and silent). End poses land through its last frame. A kept Cast voice is LTX-only. On adult stills (7 from the adult sweep) H3 rendered nude scenes without trouble and held two-person acts in 8 of 8 clips across two prompt sets where WAN held 2 of 8 (WAN's livelier motion was mostly the pair coming apart), so with H3 picked, two-person adult clips stay on it — the motion is gentler, about a third of WAN's, and the sound is breathing, no words. Download its five files under Settings → ComfyUI → Models (about 44 GB; the nvfp4 text encoder — the int8 one spilled into RAM and swap on a 24 GB card). MiniMax-H3's licence does not allow use in the US, EU, UK or South Korea, and the picker says so. Tried and left out: lightx2v's 4-step Turbo (about a quarter faster, but light streaks, less motion and a weaker face match) and H3's reference-to-video from the plate (it invents a new scene).
- **Film gaps closed.** Close-ups are widened to 16:9 too in a widescreen cut (kept on the close-up), and keep their 1.5 s when the film is fitted to a length (as do the "Previously on" shots). After a "Previously on" recap the title card comes next, not first. A film made by the browser fallback (no ffmpeg on the server) says it has no narration, music or clip sound instead of coming out silent unannounced. The garbled-writing check asks whether the text is prominent and flags only that: on 200 Day stills it flagged 8 instead of 21, nearly all real (a shop sign, a marquee, jerseys), where small background text — garbled in most generated pictures and unnoticed — used to make up most flags.
- **Film round: widescreen, close-ups, narration, sound, review links, "Previously on", sharper delivery check.** _Widescreen film_ (Day → Advanced): each still is widened to 16:9 by one Edit 2511 pass that continues the scene to the sides (live 4 of 4 believable, her size and pose kept, no white), kept on the still, and used both as the clip's start frame and for stills in a widescreen cut — no more letterboxed portrait shots. _Add close-ups_ on a card's sheet: a face close-up and a detail shot (hands, what she holds) of the same moment, cut in for 1.5 s right after it without a caption (8 of 8 live kept face, hair, dress, place and light). _Narration_ on a card's sheet: a voice-over line recorded when the film is cut (LTX-2.5 audio only, in the Cast's kept voice through the ID-LoRA when there is one) and mixed in from that shot's start, ducking the music — both live lines said word for word; the kept voice moved pitch toward the sample on one line and overshot on the other. _Give the N silent clips sound_ under the cut's readiness notes runs Add voice on every silent (WAN) clip. _Share for review_ under a film (Cast → Films): a read-only link that expires in 1–30 days, plays that one film to anyone without an account, takes comments pinned to a moment, and can be stopped. _Previously on_ (cut options, from the second episode of a season): the last episode's first, middle and last shots open the film, 2 s each. The pre-cut check also flags near-duplicate shots and, on cuts of up to 16 shots, garbled lettering (signs, labels) read by the vision model.
- **Day checks a Cast's marks and repairs them.** When the Cast has glasses or a tattoo that should show, each solo still is looked at by the vision model (asked neutrally what it sees, like the shoe check) and a miss — glasses gone, the tattoo missing, or the tattoo spread to the other arm — gets one short Edit 2511 repair pass, swapped in like Face finish (a second if the recheck still fails). Calibrated on 14 stills: glasses read right 14 of 14; lace-up panels and a mole are not counted as ink. Live, one pass fixed 3 of 4 stills with pose and place kept. _Check marks_ in Day's Advanced drawer. If the vision model can't load while ComfyUI holds the GPU, ComfyUI is asked to free memory between jobs and the check tries once more. Also on every solo still, whether or not Auto-review is on: its face match is kept on the Gallery entry, so the Cast's Likeness chart fills itself. Cast home: _Find earlier outfits_ fills the Wardrobe from earlier Day stills (a garment photo when there was one, else the words); _Compare the looks' plates_ shows how far each look's plate face is from the Default look's (on a real Cast, four looks scored 4–20% — different people). Tried and left out: a height / build line for two-person stills — on Edit 2511, "a head taller" and its reverse changed nothing (8 duos × 3).
- **More on Cast home.** _Bible → Marks_: tattoos, scars, moles, freckles, glasses — said in a Day still when that part of them shows (an arm tattoo stays out under long sleeves), and painted onto the look's plate by _Prepare plate → Her marks_ (one Edit 2511 pass, the rest kept). Words alone did nothing: no glasses on 0 of 4 replayed stills on Edit 2511 or Rapid; with the marked plate, glasses held 8 of 8 — a tattoo shows but tends to spread to the other arm, so the line now says "no other ink" and the negative keeps extra tattoos out. _Props_: their phone, bag, car, dog… described once, said whenever a beat mentions it. _People in their life_: partner, sibling, best friend, rival… set once for both; two-person Day stills say how the pair relate (siblings aren't drawn as a date), and family never plays the partner in Suggestive, Date night or the adult moods. _How they talk_: sounds-like spellings for words the clip voice says wrong (only the clip prompt changes — the line and captions keep the real spelling), and their own lines as one-tap chips beside a clip's line. _Overview → Likeness_: each look's face match over time with plate changes marked and a warning on a drop; _Measure_ scores stills that have none (on ComfyUI's CPU, no render job) — until now scores were only kept when Day's review ran. _Wardrobe_: every outfit a Day still was made in, with _Wear again_. _Reference sheet_: three-quarter, side, back and four expressions of the plate (seven Edit 2511 edits, all seven right on a live plate), printable as a character card. The LoRA flywheel checks the keepers first (how many, how many places, how much they look like the Cast). Fix: "clean unmarked skin" no longer fights a Cast's scar, mole or freckles.
- **Setup without secrets in the env file.** A new install needs no `openssl rand` lines: the session secret is made on first start and kept in the data folder, and with no `PROMPT_ADMIN_PASSWORD` the sign-in page asks for the admin's username and password on the first visit (on a server other machines can reach, plus a one-time setup code from the server log — `docker compose logs castcut`). In Docker the `.env` file is now optional and sign-in is on by default. Behind a reverse proxy that sends `X-Forwarded-Host` (Caddy, Traefik, or nginx with one line) `PROMPT_TRUSTED_HOSTS` is no longer needed; the proxy's client address is used for rate limits when sent (`TRUST_PROXY_HEADERS` is now auto); emails link to the address the admin last signed in at instead of `127.0.0.1` unless `PROMPT_API_URL` pins one. Fixes: a password changed in the app was put back to `PROMPT_ADMIN_PASSWORD` on every start (now only when the env value changes); the sign-in check kept an old account list as long as the app kept polling (its 2 s cache never expired while busy), so an account made elsewhere couldn't sign in. The LLM and ComfyUI tabs end with what differs from the defaults there, each with a reset. Signing in under `pnpm run dev` from the LAN address or a proxy name works (dev served its scripts only to localhost), and after a successful sign-in the button stays busy until the next page opens.
- **The LLM model is one the server has.** With no `LLM_MODEL` set, Castcut wrote with a built-in "dolphin-llama3" — on an LM Studio that did not have it every LLM call failed, while setup showed the LLM (and vision, same name) green. It now writes with a text model the server lists, and setup shows the models really used (vision: the one image checks pick), or says when a configured model is not on the server.
- **Simpler first run and Settings.** The welcome is one screen — _Make my first film_, _Try a starter film_, or just an image — instead of three steps with nine choices and a density picker. A fresh install no longer announces "ComfyUI's models changed — mapped 68 new entries". _Heal & ready_ lives once, on Overview (its film checks folded under it); the ComfyUI tab's essentials view keeps the address and leaves the machine pool, every-model overrides and manual injection to _Show all ComfyUI settings_. The Data tab leads with Backup & move and disk space, the rest folded under _More_. Overview's pointer-only Appearance card is gone (About says where theme lives).
- **Import a script into Day.** Under the idea row, _Import a script…_ takes a pasted (or opened) Fountain screenplay or a plain outline: each scene becomes a card — the heading's place ("INT. KITCHEN - MORNING" → kitchen), the first action as the beat (the lead's name turned to she / he, as Day's beats name nobody) and the lead's first line as the card's line — previewed before it replaces the board, up to 8 cards (the Day grows to fit). No LLM. The board's old stills leave the cards (asked first; they stay in the Gallery). Also: typed beats (and imported ones) are no longer silently rerolled when the board doesn't match the mood.
- **Engine for this shot.** A Day card's sheet has _Engine for this shot_: the still renders on another of Day's engines (Rapid AIO, Edit 2511, Qwen-Image 2.1) while the rest of the Day keeps its own — a pose the Day's engine keeps missing, redone alone. Only installed engines are offered; it wins over the automatic hand-offs (adult nude → Rapid, clothed duo on 2.1 → Rapid, best engine per pose).
- **Export for an editor.** A film's _What's in it · Re-cut · Export_ (Cast page → Films) now downloads a zip for DaVinci Resolve, Premiere Pro or Final Cut: an FCPXML timeline (each shot in order with its trims and holds, the music under the cut), a CMX 3600 EDL, the captions as SRT subtitles, and the shots themselves — clips converted to H.264 MP4 (WAN's animated WebP imports nowhere), stills to PNG. Crossfades, the title card and still motion stay Castcut's; the README says how to add a dissolve.

## [v2.8.0] - 2026-10-14

- **No more third hands or legs on solo adult stills.** Three causes, all words fighting the pose map: (1) a pose that holds her up (all fours, bent over, flat on her stomach, leaning back on a hand) still got "one hand between her thighs, the other on her breast" — the map's supporting arm made a third hand; the hands line now braces one hand (all fours, laundry prone: third hand 3/3 and 2/3 → 0/3 each on the same seeds). (2) The pose redo's "left arm down, not bent at the elbow" fought the beat's "her left hand cups her breast" — the redo now leaves arms out when the words give the hands a job (Day automatic and Look-wrong redos, Story). (3) Sitting on the bed edge said "thighs spread" while the map hangs her shins down: legs up on the bed plus the map's leg; it now says the map's legs ("lower legs hanging down … two legs only", 3/3 clean, toy held in one hand).
- **Cast bundle.** The Cast file carried one picture (the active plate): every other look came in plate-less, and dress plates, films and stills stayed behind. Cast home → More → _Export bundle_ writes one .tar with the Cast, its Story and Day plan, every look's plate and face, earlier plates, the dress plates made from them, and its films — its stills too when ticked — with their Gallery entries. _Import a Cast file or bundle_ on the Cast list unpacks it (in pieces, like the Pictures backup), adds the Cast and its entries, and names any picture that did not come along. Checked between two installs (one signed in, one not): all four of a Cast's looks arrived with their plates.
- **A replay that needs a missing picture says so.** Replaying or re-rolling an older still sends its exact graph, which loads the plate, face crop, pose guide or dress plate it was made from. When one of those was gone from ComfyUI's input folder (cleaned up, or another ComfyUI), ComfyUI refused with a bare _Invalid image file_. Before queueing, each picture a graph loads is now asked for (HEAD /view), and a missing one is named, with the way back: a Pictures backup, or rendering the shot again from its card. Models, LoRAs and node types were already checked against ComfyUI before queueing. Skipped with a pool of ComfyUIs (the job may go to another host).
- **Earlier plates.** Replacing a look's plate (upload, Gallery pick, Generate, Prepare, Look's extract) or removing it could not be undone, except Prepare's own Undo. Each look now keeps its last six plates, each with the face that went with it: Cast home → Looks → _Earlier plates_ → _Restore_ puts one back, and the current plate takes its place in the list.
- **Generate a look's plate on Cast home.** The Looks block offered only an upload or a Gallery pick (Look's generator sat a page away). _Generate plate_ (or _Generate new plate_ on a look that has one) renders it from the Cast's description and face, the same plate Day's _Make a plate from the description_ makes, with an optional line for what the look changes (hair, makeup). The current plate stays until the new one lands, and it lands on the look that asked even if another look is picked meanwhile.
- **Make it 30 s on WAN keeps its detail and colour.** Each WAN part started from the last one's softest frame, and WAN renders softer than what it starts from, so detail compounded away (172 → 30 by 14 s on a live 3-part chain). Each part's start frame is now sharpened back toward the clip's own detail, never past it (84 at 14 s, no halos). The per-part colour match had also never run on WAN: ffmpeg cannot seek into WAN's animated WebP, the middle-frame grab wrote nothing and the error was swallowed. Grabbing by decoding fixes it — colour drift by the end 3.1 → 0.5.

## [v2.7.3] - 2026-10-14

- **Deleting Gallery entries frees their disk space.** Delete, _Purge_ and _Archive & purge_ removed the entry and ComfyUI's history, but the server's kept copy (`gallery-media/<owner>/<entry>/`) stayed forever: a real install had 6,094 copies for 135 entries, 13.7 GB of 14. Deleting now removes the copies, except ones a Day / Story still or Cast plate still links to. _Settings → Data → Gallery disk space → Find old copies_ lists what earlier deletes left (on that install 4,831 copies, 11.5 GB) and removes them after a confirm: copies still in a Gallery or linked from saved data on the server or in this browser stay, as do copies newer than 7 days and Cast plate copies. ComfyUI's own outputs are not touched. The same panel can clear the thumbnail cache, which already trims itself (24 h, 1 GB).
- **Docker setup in one folder.** `docker/compose.yml` plus one settings file: `cp .env.example .env`, fill in ComfyUI, the LLM and a sign-in password, `docker compose up -d --build`. The old compose had two copies of the app (the default one could not start: the image binds `0.0.0.0`, which the startup check counts as exposed with sign-in off), forced Ollama on, and kept no data. Now: one `castcut` service whose published address (`CASTCUT_BIND`) is also what the startup check reads (`PROMPT_BIND_HOST`); data in the `castcut_data` volume (`PROMPT_DATA_DIR=/data`, set in the image); `host.docker.internal` works on Linux; Ollama and ComfyUI are opt-in profiles. The build context also skips worktrees, venvs and LoRAs (it sent over 40 GB from a dev checkout).
- **Pictures backup.** _Settings → Data → Download / Restore pictures backup_ carries the files the JSON backup only names: the Gallery's kept copies, the identity lock, and the ComfyUI pictures the Cast and plates use (112 on a real install). Download streams one .tar; restore uploads it in 32 MB pieces (fits the 80 MB request cap and reverse proxies), files Gallery copies under the signed-in account (a signed-in server's backup restores into one without sign-in), uploads missing pictures to this server's ComfyUI under their own names, and skips what is already there.
- **Docker: media from the right ComfyUI.** Gallery entries and old settings carry the default `http://127.0.0.1:8188`; in a container that address is the container itself, so pictures and queues failed. A loopback ComfyUI address from the browser now gives way to the server's own non-loopback `COMFYUI_API_URL`.
- **`crypto.randomUUID is not a function`** over plain http at a LAN or Docker host name (browsers only offer it on HTTPS or localhost): making a look and ~20 other actions failed. It is filled in at page load.
- **Signing in leaves the login form.** On an iPhone sign-in worked but stayed on the form: the app reused the pages it had fetched while signed out (each a redirect to the login). Sign-in now loads the next page in full.
- **Phones open the phone app.** A phone browser asking for a desk page is sent to its /m twin (`/` → Film, `/day` → phone Day); iPads and tablets keep the desk. _Desk_ on the phone is remembered, and _Phone view_ (Film's More menu on small screens, the Phone button, the banner) goes back. The Film kiosk had no way to the phone app at all.
- **Render previews survive a reverse proxy.** Behind Cloudflare a job waiting in ComfyUI's queue left the preview stream silent, the proxy closed it after ~100 s and that render showed no preview. The stream now sends a keep-alive line every 20 s, and a stream that drops after it opened is opened again (up to 5 times) without an error.
- **Queue day clears every card's old line.** A new still over a finished one clears the card's _Line in the clip_, tone and reply, but in Queue day each card saved the plan as it was before the run, putting back the lines the cards before it had just cleared (only the last card lost its old line). The still queue now works from the latest cards.
- **She eats with a fork.** "Sitting at a dinner table twirling pasta" named no fork, and a Day still fed her spaghetti by hand. An eating beat that names no utensil now gets one for that still — a fork for pasta, salad, cake or steak, chopsticks for noodles, ramen or sushi, a spoon for soup or cereal; finger food (pizza, a sandwich, a croissant) and cereal straight from the box are left alone. The built-in dinner beat now says _twirling spaghetti on her fork_.
- **Switching the idea row from _One event_ to _Whole day_ leaves the event.** It does what _Suggest day_ does — fresh whole-day scenes on every card, the event gone — instead of only changing the tab while the event ran on underneath. Checked in the app with a reload.

## [v2.7.2] - 2026-10-13

- **Fix: the rest of One event's leaks into a whole day.** Changing the number of stills or the mood, or _Reroll plan_ on a card, also left the event on underneath — a re-rolled card was still made as an edit of the one before (a rooftop ledge drawn over the stairs picture, an old event's towel and book still in her hands). They now leave the event too, and as a safety net a Day only runs as an event while every card still carries its event move, so an event left behind can't chain edits, hold back Auto-review or join clips. Checked in the app with a reload after each.

## [v2.7.1] - 2026-10-13

- **Fix: One event's rules carried on into a whole day.** Switching the idea row to _Whole day_ and pressing _Write the day_ (or _Suggest day_) rewrote the cards but left the event on underneath, so the new whole day still queued each card as a same-camera edit of the one before, only flagged Auto-review misses and joined the clips. Both now leave the event the way _Back to a whole day_ does — checked in the app, including after a reload.

## [v2.7.0] - 2026-10-13

- **One event (Day).** Switch Day's idea row to _One event_ and write one line — _a quiet afternoon reading at the beach_ — and every slot becomes a consecutive moment of that event in one place, filmed from one fixed camera: Queue day makes the first moment the usual Day way and each later one as a same-camera edit of the one before, and Animate ends each clip on the next moment's still, so Cut plays the clips as one continuous video. The LLM plans it to stay filmable: one spot and one posture (no lying down, walking off or leaving the picture), nobody else in the picture (waiters, dates, passers-by and cyclists were painted in when named), one or two props held from first to last with a fixed description (no picking up or putting down — where props were invented, doubled or lost), and every move and the ending something her hands do, so the clips never stall. A plan that falls short goes back to the LLM with what is wrong (19 of 20 varied events planned in about 2 tries). Moment 1 is settled to its own beat; the last clip moves into an edit of the ending, with scene words cut and a drifted ending dropped. Auto-review redos only flag on an event Day (a redone moment broke the chain). _Back to a whole day_ restores the Day's own scenes. Tested end to end in the app on beach reading, painting, café coffee, cooking, knitting, watering plants, rooftop wine and journal writing: same place and props throughout, the largest step between clips 0.9–1.1 grey levels in the last round. Needs the LLM; clothed moods only. Rough edges are left (an occasional LTX glitter or stray gesture) — re-roll a moment or fix it in post.
- **Docs site: no more broken links.** The home page's Pick your path links and the links from Features, Architecture, Prompt limits, Desktop and Performance to files outside the docs (the main README, `desktop/README.md`, CI and config files) went to 404s on GitHub Pages; they now open the right page or the file on GitHub. Pose reference credits drop the links to five deleted Flickr accounts and a closed Google+ profile (the names stay), and a removed Flickr photo points at its archived copy — in the docs and in the app's pose credits. The docs build now runs `--strict`, so a broken link or heading anchor fails it.
- **Gender swap on the Cast bible picture.** Cast → Bible: _Gender swap_ beside _Picture this bible_ edits the rendered picture (Edit 2511) into the Cast's other-gender version — same scene, pose, outfit and camera — and shows it beside the original (also in the gallery). Live: two women → men (4 of 4 read as the same person) and a man → woman in the app. A new bible picture drops the old swap.
- **Day clips default to LTX-2.5.** Day's clips followed Video → Model (WAN by default); they now run on LTX-2.5 except two-person adult clips (WAN unless _Use LTX-2.5 for two-person adult clips too_). _Day clips on → WAN_ in the engine note under the cards switches Day back to WAN; talking clips stay on LTX-2.5 either way. Story still follows Video → Model.
- **Fix: End pose on WAN never reached the end picture.** Day clips are 64 frames; WAN renders 1 + 4n, so it made 61 and dropped the end frame — the clip ended where it started and cut hard to the next shot. End-pose clips now run 65 frames (last frame vs end picture 50 → 29 grey levels apart, the pose landing on it).
- **Fix: Re-pose this still came back square.** A portrait still's re-pose edit rendered on the square sidebar canvas (960×1280 → 1328², wider), so a clip couldn't move into it; it now renders at the still's own shape.
- **A new picture on a Day card clears its line.** Rendering a new still over a finished one empties the card's spoken line, how it's said and the reply — they were written for the old picture. _Keep the full frame_ stays. A card's first still keeps a line written ahead of time.
- **Fix: the Cast's name painted into a try-on.** Image briefs (Outfit try-on, Day, Look) said `subject: <name>`; an unusual name ("Gloovi the Cap-Weaver") came out as a logo panel beside her. Identity comes from the plate image, so briefs no longer name the Cast.
- **Talking clips no longer freeze arms or legs mid-action.** A still caught mid-action — arms overhead in a stretch, a jump shot's ball, a golf follow-through, a knee lifted in a dance, a lunge, bent over picking something up, mid-stride — kept that pose frozen for the whole line, or (with the old "finish the movement and settle" wording) turned round and walked off. Which limbs are mid-action is now read from the still's pose, not just its words (half of them said nothing a word rule could catch), and the clip holds her spot with one exception per limb: she lowers the raised arm, puts the lifted foot down, stands evenly on both feet, straightens up — facing the same way, not stepping toward the camera. A foot resting on a step or a wall is left as it is. Bent over on straight legs is no longer read as seated (she squatted down). Across 35 of the user's stills at the app's size on fresh seeds: limbs left frozen 40 of 105 clips → 5, anything off 60 → 24. Chest-up talking clips lower a raised arm or a camera held to the eye too, and keep looking at the camera after the line (31 → 26 of 64). A crouch (a volleyball ready stance) that nothing else caught now rises and stands — held 2 of 3 before, 0 of 3 after.
- **Fix: a standing still read as "mid" no longer sits her down.** The seated hold was chosen by how big her face was in the frame, and a standing, waving still at the farmers market came out sitting. Seated is now read from the still's pose (both thighs near level, or lying — the park bench, a record shop, a library read seated; standing, waving and stair-climbing stills don't), else from its words; only then the seated hold. Re-rendered: standing on 2 of 2 (the camera still drifts in a little at the end).

## [v2.6.0] - 2026-10-11

- **Security audit for running behind a reverse proxy.** Signed-out visitors used to get internal service addresses, model names, GPU, versions and data paths from `/api/health`, and the admin username from `/api/auth/session` — now only _is it up_. Sign-in had no brute-force limit beyond the general 120 requests a minute (and behind a proxy every visitor shared it): 5 failed sign-ins (or authenticator codes) for a username now lock it for 15 minutes, 20 per client address, and an unknown username takes as long to refuse as a real one. Rate limits use the address the proxy saw (`X-Real-IP`, else the last `X-Forwarded-For` entry), not one the visitor wrote. The server no longer fetches private or local addresses for shot and webhook URLs a signed-in user supplies (checked after DNS resolution; the configured ComfyUI and the app itself by exact origin); a request may not name its own ComfyUI address while login is on unless `COMFYUI_ALLOW_CLIENT_URL` says so; a signed-in user no longer falls back to the shared `_global` gallery. Pages send `X-Frame-Options`, `frame-ancestors 'self'`, `nosniff`, a referrer policy and no `X-Powered-By`. Dependencies: `sharp` 0.35.5 everywhere (libvips / libheif / librsvg CVEs on uploaded images), `nodemailer` 10.1 — production dependencies audit clean. New _Running behind a reverse proxy_ checklist in docs/configuration.md.
- **Fix: Castcut at the machine's IP address, or behind a reverse proxy, refused every request.** The production server compared the browser's origin with its own `localhost` address, so a page opened at `http://192.168.x.x:47832` (even `127.0.0.1`) got _Unauthorized_ on every call — ComfyUI looked unreachable — and a login through a reverse proxy was refused. IP addresses and `localhost` now count as the app's own address; a proxy's public name goes in the new `PROMPT_TRUSTED_HOSTS` (other names aren't trusted, so a DNS-rebinding page still can't get in). The session cookie is `Secure` only over HTTPS, so logging in over plain HTTP sticks.
- **Two-person adult clips can run on LTX-2.5.** Under Animate, with LTX-2.5 picked: _Use LTX-2.5 for two-person adult clips too_ (off by default — they stay on WAN). Plain LTX moved about half as much as WAN on the user's stills and drifted from the act to a kiss on one. Tuned with the user over a day of A/Bs: they run at the same 24 fps as every other LTX clip with a gentle tempo line (conditioning at 12 fps matched WAN's pace, but the user preferred LTX's own; a stronger line overshot to 2–5× and looked sped up); an anatomy line plus holding the still harder on the first pass (0.85 instead of 0.7) keeps genitals one consistent shape frame to frame instead of blobbing and merging; and it renders at 1024 on the long side (768 hid the detail, 1152 was no steadier; ~38 s a clip). Across 10 stills — making love, reverse cowgirl, straddling, oral, face-down, kneeling face to face, mating press, standing — it held the act every time, where WAN drifted on 2 of 6. Add sound gives them breathing and the room.
- **Room sound under talking clips** (on by default — _Room sound under the voice_, under a slot's or scene's line; needs castcut-nodes 1.7.0 in ComfyUI). Asking for room sound in the talking prompt did nothing, so it gets its own sound-only pass of the place. The first version listened to the talking picture, read her lips and spoke a second, garbled line louder than hers (the user's night clip); it now listens to a blurred copy (no words in the room track on 3 of the user's clips; a frozen still gave silence), and the new **Castcut Audio Match Level** node sets it 20 dB under her spoken level (15 sounded a little loud to the user) — the raw pass varied by 30 dB from clip to clip. The line still transcribed word for word. Shops, cafés, plazas and more have their own sounds; a place the pass leaves silent stays silent.
- **Talking clips from a still caught mid-movement settle first.** A stride, a spin or a dance step was held frozen for the whole line (or she walked on into a close-up); now she finishes the movement and stands, balanced, then talks — wide and standing on 8 of 8 (walking, spinning and dancing stills). Climbing stairs is left as it was (settling there turned her round and up them).
- **Fix: talking clips from standing stills no longer sit her down.** The pose hold added for the zoom fix ("sitting or lying, she stays that way") sat standing women down on the pavement in 6 of 6 replays (coffee, fountain, harbor stills). Wide (full-body) talking clips now hold "exactly the pose and position of the first frame" without posture words — standing 8 of 8 — and the seated wording stays for mid (seated / knees-up) stills, where it kept the park-bench clip full-frame.
- **Talking clips keep the scene alive.** Steam from a coffee, candle flames, a fountain, water, leaves, rain or snow, people in the background — named from the place and the spoken line — keep moving while she talks (the user's coffee clip: the steam never moved; with the cue it rose and curled on 2 of 2 seeds).
- **Refine detail (opt-in, under Animate).** LTX-2.5 clips get one more pass through Lightricks' Refine-Details IC-LoRA, guided by the clip's own frames: crisper texture, about 2× the time, and it can shift her face a little (frame-1 face distance 0.28 → 0.68 on one test still). Talking clips keep their voice (the refine sees it frozen; the MP4 audio is byte-identical) and their lip timing (mouth movement 0.97–1.00 correlated with the unrefined clip, 4 clips). Needs `ltx-2.5-22b-ic-lora-refine-details-1.0.safetensors` (Hugging Face, license click-through) and a working ComfyUI-LTXVideo pack; without them the clip renders as before.
- **LTX-2.5 is now the clip engine for everything but two-person adult clips.** With LTX-2.5 picked, clothed one-person clips no longer fall back to WAN (they kept her face closer on LTX — see below); untick _Use LTX-2.5 for clothed one-person clips_ under Animate to send them to WAN. Two-person adult clips stay on WAN unless _Use LTX-2.5 for two-person adult clips too_ is ticked. Text-to-video and extend still run on WAN (LTX-2.5 here is image-to-video only).
- **Clothed one-person clips on LTX-2.5 keep her face closer.** They now hold the still harder (0.85) and render at 1024. Scored against the still's face on 8 of the user's stills (lower = closer): WAN 0.79, plain LTX 0.71, held LTX 0.55 (worst frame 0.90 / 0.82 / 0.71). One of the 8 walked away down the stairs instead of climbing them.
- **One-person adult clips on LTX-2.5 get the same tuning** (calm tempo line, firmer still hold, 1024; no two-person anatomy line). On four solo stills: plain LTX lost her anatomy over the last frames of one clip where the tuned one kept it, was clearer on another, and the tuned clips moved like WAN where plain LTX overshot (60 vs WAN's 37).
- **Fix: two-person adult LTX clips now get the settings tuned for them.** The firmer still hold and 1024 size were dropped on the way to the queue, so only their prompt lines had applied.
- **Animate asks what should happen.** Animate on a Day slot (the card's ⋯ menu or the slot sheet, desk and phone) opens a sheet: the engine the clip runs on (WAN Rapid AIO, LTX-2.5…) and _What happens in the clip_, prefilled with the slot's beat. Type your own direction in plain words — it goes through the same template the beat would (the clothed-clip template, or the fixed intimate template on adult moods), so it's formatted for the engine; _What gets sent_ shows the prompt as you type, _Back to the beat_ restores the default. Talking slots say their line instead. _Animate all_ still runs straight away.
- **Four more ways to say a line: Nervous, Scared, Shouting, Sing-song.** Tested on one still and line, same seed, against Natural (≈195 Hz, −15.7 LUFS, 60% voiced): Nervous 252 Hz with a wide wobble and 35% voiced (hesitant breaks); Scared louder (−12.9) and faster; Shouting +3.5 LU at 380 Hz ending on "!"; Sing-song 278 Hz, 80% voiced, slower — every clip said the words exactly. Sad, Sleepy, Confident and Sarcastic were tried too (two seeds, two wordings each) and did not change the delivery consistently, so they are not offered.
- **"How it's said" is a dropdown.** Under a line on Day and Story (and beside each line in _Make it 30 s_, where it now shows from the start instead of only after a line was typed).
- **Add sound: the place, not just "room sound".** _Add voice_ is now _Add sound_ (it never added words — a dub can't move lips): a silent clip gets the sound of its place — the slot's Setting (Story's Setting) with its likely sounds: waves and gulls at a beach, birds and a breeze in a park, murmur and cups at a café, traffic in a street, keyboards in an office, a quiet room with a clock at home, hushed late at night. On a beach still, same seed: the old prompt gave a low tonal drone; the new one a broadband wash of waves and wind, no words.
- **Fix: talking clips still punched in on seated stills.** Keep the full frame only protected full-body stills (face under 8% of the height); a park-bench still at ~8% got the plain "looks into the camera" wording and LTX cut to a face close-up within 2 s. Stills kept whole up to 16% face height — and stills where no face is found — now get the wide-shot wording too. Replaying that still with an angry line, 4 seeds: the wide wording held the frame in 2 of 4 (she stood up in the others), a "medium-wide, same distance" wording in 0 of 4 — and the wide wording plus "in the same pose as the first frame — sitting or lying, she stays that way" held seated and full-frame in 4 of 4, which is what ships.
- **Fix: Studio's sidebar sometimes lost the film card.** It read the Cast once at first paint; when the Cast list loaded a moment later it stayed hidden. It now follows the Cast list and settings as they load.
- **Fix: "Clip unavailable" on Day after a reload.** A clip that failed to load once — the first request after a reload, a busy ComfyUI — stayed "Clip unavailable" on its card while it played in the lightbox. The card now retries (twice, with a short back-off), retries once more on its own after a few seconds, and has _Try again_.
- **Preview the cut.** _Cut options → ▶ Preview the cut_ plays the film as it will come out, before any encode: shots in your order, holds (fitted to the music or a set length, on the beat), clip trims, waist-up / chest-up framing, the slow zoom, crossfades, captions and the title card, in the cut's shape (16:9 letterboxed, 9:16 and 1:1 crop-filled) — with the music and the lines at their levels, the music ducked under lines. Play / Pause and a scrub bar. It uses the encodes' own timing and drawing, so what you see is what you cut. (Levels above 100% play at 100% in the preview.)
- **Auto-review stops re-rolling good outfits.** When Auto-review says a one-person still is in the wrong outfit, it now takes a second look before re-rolling: it describes her clothes plainly and checks that loosely against the plan. A tuxedo seen as "blazer and trousers", cycling kit as "black top and shorts", a samurai set as "kimono" no longer cost a re-roll. Measured on 30 of your Day stills, each also paired with another still's outfit: the old one-step check flagged 7 of 30 correctly dressed stills (and caught 25 of 30 wrong outfits); with the second look, 0 of 30 false alarms and 20 of 30 caught. A miss that stands now says what she is wearing: _outfit does not match — she is in white floral nightie_.
- **Fix: Day and Story clips on "WAN Video" sampled like full WAN 2.2.** Castcut's clip graph loads the Rapid AIO file for every WAN engine, but plain _WAN Video_ (also the fallback when LTX-2.5 can't take a shot) used full-WAN sampling — Max = 40 steps at CFG 5 with uni_pc on a CFG-1 model: ~10× slower and overcooked. Those clips now sample as Rapid AIO (Max = 8 steps, CFG 1, euler_ancestral / beta).
- **Checked before the cut: files, black frames, sound.** Pressing _Cut film_ (Day and Story) now reads each shot's file first ("Checking the shots…"): a file that's gone, a still that is black, a clip that is mostly black or goes black for over 1.5 s, and a talking clip with no sound or a silent track ("its line won't be heard") join the face / pose / Auto-review list in _N shots need a look_ — Retry, Leave them out, or Cut anyway. A shot ComfyUI can't be reached for says so, rather than calling the file missing. Measured on real media through the server's ffmpeg: a black PNG, a silent track (−91 dB), a clip without audio and a 2.5 s black gap were each caught; the night sample still (brightness 118 of 255) and a normal talking clip (−12.8 dB) were not.
- **What the cut will quietly do, said up front.** Under _Ready to cut_, Day lists what would otherwise happen silently — _Evening: the still failed — left out_, _Morning: the clip failed — its still is used_, _Afternoon: its line won't be heard (no talking clip)_ — and every Cut (Day and Story) says which checks have run on the shots going in: _Checked: faces 3 of 4, auto-review · Not checked: pose_.
- **Looks like the Cast.** Each finished Day still has a face mark — _Looks like Nora 72%_, _Face may drift 48%_ (amber) or _Not Nora? 31%_ (red) — from its measured face match, and the Cut shot list shows the same mark per shot (Day and Story; Story uses its own, lower bars for staged scenes). Before, the face match showed only in the Gallery and on Story scenes.
- **Trim clips in the cut.** The Cut shot list has _from … to … of 6.2 s_ on every clip: the cut plays only that piece, its voice trimmed with it, in both the server and the browser encode; the length fit and _Score this film_ count the trimmed length. Checked on a server encode: a 1 s → 3 s trim played exactly source seconds 1–3.
- **Square and 1080p cuts.** _Cut options_ has **Shape** — Widescreen 16:9, Vertical 9:16 or Square 1:1 (new, crop-filled like vertical, for feed posts) — and **Size**, 720p or 1080p (Day and Story only cut 720p before). The poster follows the shape.
- **Closer framing for a still, by crop.** Each still in the Cut shot list has _frame: as shot / waist up / chest up_: the still is cropped around her face (3:4, never under 420 px tall, so a tiny face gets a looser crop rather than mush) before either encoder sees it; a still whose face can't be found stays as shot and the cut says so. Prompts can't do this: asked for "a close-up from the chest up" or "the frame cuts off at her waist", Edit 2511 kept her thighs in frame on all four replays of a seated vacation still (2026-10-11).
- **Pick the poster, even from a clip.** _Poster_ on any shot in the shot list makes Save poster use it; on a clip, _frame at … s_ picks the moment (default: the trim's in point). Before, the poster was always the first still.
- **Sound mix.** _Music_ (0–150%) and _Lines_ (50–200%) levels under the audio bed set how loud the music and the talking clips' voices sit in the cut; the music still ducks under every line. Server encode only (the browser fallback cuts picture only).
- **What the Day holds the same.** Above the Day's clothing a _Held across the Day_ row says what every still keeps and what each is free to change: _Face · plate + face finish_ (or _no plate_), _Clothes · Sage slip dress (1 slot differs)_ (or _per slot_), _Place · Brick loft home (2)_ (the Cast's recurring places, or _each setting its own_), _Weather · rain_ (or _Light · each slot its own_) — and _free in each still: pose, setting, beat_. After the stills land, the face mark on each card checks the face; clothes and places are not measured.
- **How long a batch will take.** Under Day's buttons: _Queue the rest: 2 stills · ~1 min each · done in ~3 min · 1 job ahead_ and _Animate all: 3 clips · ~4 min each · …_, timed from this machine's own recent renders (or "no clip timed on this machine yet"), on the desk and the phone Day.
- **Preview the mix.** Under the audio bed, _▶ Preview the mix_ plays the music and every talking clip's line together in the browser, at the _Music_ / _Lines_ levels, each line where the encode will put it (same timing as the server mix: trims, crossfades, title card) and the music ducked under it — before spending an encode. Sound only.
- **Every film remembers how it was cut — re-cut it in another shape.** A cut film (Day, Story, episode, Cast film) keeps its recipe on its Gallery entry: the shots in order with their trims, framing, captions and holds, the music and its levels, the length and title options. On the Cast page each such film has _What's in it · Re-cut_: its shots listed, and **Re-cut** at another shape and size (a widescreen Day as a 1080p vertical for Reels, say) — saved as a new film beside it, without opening the Day or Story again. Shots that lived only in the browser (demo stills, uploads) can't come back and are counted.
- **Undo / redo for the Day plan.** Beside the plan, _↶ Undo Afternoon's beat_ / _↷ Redo_ step back through edits to places, beats, outfits, lines, poses and the number of stills (a burst of typing is one step; up to 30). It starts fresh when the Day changes Cast, so another Cast's plan never comes back.
- **Every episode keeps its Day — open it again.** Each Day cut now saves the Day it came from on its episode (the plan, mood, weather and the finished stills and clips — server URLs only, a few KB). On the Cast page, an episode's _What went into it_ lists each slot with its still or clip, place, beat and line, and **Open this Day again** puts that Day back on the Day board. Nothing is lost doing it: the Day that was there is kept aside, and Day shows _Opened Season 1, episode 2 again — Bring back the Day you had_ (which sets the reopened one aside in turn). For a Cast that isn't the one on Day, the episode's Day waits as their parked Day and comes in when they are picked.
- **Restore shows what a backup holds first.** Every _Import / Restore backup_ (Settings → Data, Settings overview, Profile, Studio history) now reads the file and shows it beside what is here — Cast (by name), films, renders, prompt history, workflows, and when it was saved — marking anything the file has less of (_Restoring replaces what is here: the file has less of cast_). The default restore downloads a copy of what is here first. It also says how many renders in the file live only in ComfyUI's output folder (a backup holds the list, settings and Cast, not the files). Before, picking a file restored it at once.

## [v2.5.0] - 2026-10-11

- **Three levels: Film, Studio, More tools.** Profile → Appearance describes them as such (Film is the default; _Back to Film_ resets to it), and Studio's sidebar keeps the film in progress at the top — the Cast, "Film · 2 of 3 · Day", its next step and _Back to Film_ — so switching to Studio no longer loses the film.
- **Made with: change or keep.** Under each picture in a still's _Made with_: Face / Look → _Change_ opens the Cast; Clothes / Shoes → _Change_ opens the slot's clothing; Pose guide → _Keep_ pins that pose for the next take (only when the guide names a pose Day knows) and _Change_ jumps to the pose preview.
- **A real sample Day.** The welcome's sample film and Day's _Demo stills_ are four stills of the demo Cast, Nora, morning to night (a 10-second reel with slow zooms, 0.5 MB), not gradient cards.
- **Phone tabs match the desk.** Mobile Studio's dock is Film · Day · Outfit · Story, like the desktop Film dock; Look, Cast, Gallery and Queue are under More (it had seven tabs to the desk's four).
- **ComfyUI offline is said everywhere.** The header chip names the problem in words — _ComfyUI offline_ (red) or _LLM offline_ (amber: rendering works, suggestions don't) — instead of an unlabelled dot; every page shows a _ComfyUI is offline_ banner (your work is kept, _Check again_, _Connection settings_); Queue, Animate and Outfit try-ons are disabled with the reason beside them, and Story's automatic clips wait. A render queued while it is known to be down is refused with that sentence, and a non-JSON answer from in between (a proxy, a crashed server) reads as words, not "Unexpected token … is not valid JSON". Before, Day said "Ready to cut" with a green "plate ready" and live buttons.
- **Lost jobs stop "running" forever.** A job ComfyUI has in neither its queue nor its history (checked twice, a moment apart) now ends as _Job not found — ComfyUI no longer has it… Queue it again_, with Retry; jobs that arrive by server sync are checked too. The demo install had three jobs "Running" for a week, with a Cancel button.
- **One film progress, Cast → Day → Cut film.** The header, the step strip, the Play Steps list, the Play page's button and the Cast page's _What's next_ now read the same model: three numbered steps (Cast, Day, Cut film) with Look, Outfit and Story named as optional and never counted ("Film · 2 of 3 · Day"). Before, the header said "2 of 2", the strip numbered five steps with Story but no Cut, the Steps list highlighted the optional Look under a header saying _Continue to Day_, and _What's next_ pointed at "Outfit kept" after a film was cut. A film saved for another Cast no longer shows as the picked Cast's progress, and the page re-reads it once server sync lands.
- **Made with.** A finished still's slot sheet (Day) or scene sheet (Story) shows what it was made from — Face, Look, Clothes, Shoes, Partner, Pose guide, End pose — read off the still's own graph, plus its Place.
- **Fixes:** Outfit's kit search was squeezed to ~40 px under the clothing-type filter; footwear _Browse_ fell onto a line of its own; focused inputs and dropdowns had no focus ring with reduced motion; the welcome stepper failed an accessibility check; Settings showed SMTP red on every local install; the phone Film nudge named the last film's lead instead of the picked Cast and counted "188 hours"; plain words for the adult-moods switch, Face finish's skip reason and _Heal & ready_; the welcome screen says up front whether ComfyUI is ready.
- **Bring your own voice.** Cast → Voice has _Use my own recording_ (any audio or video file — WAV, MP3, M4A, WebM, a phone video) and _Record_ (6 s from the microphone, where the browser allows it): the silence before the first word is skipped, the level evened out, and the first 5 s of speech kept as the Cast's voice sample, used like a kept audition (Deeper / Higher, _Steer talking clips_). Under 3 s of speech is refused with a note to record about 5 s.
- **Fix: Keep the full frame zoomed in to her face.** On a full-body still kept whole, LTX-2.5 punched in to a face close-up within about 1.5 s — 7 of 8 same-seed replays of a Day florist clip, whatever the camera wording said (stronger "no zoom" words and an end-frame guide did not help; the guide only dissolved back out). A full-body still (face under 8% of the height) now gets a wide-shot talking prompt that does not have her address the camera: 8 of 8 held the wide framing. With an _Excited_ line she can still stand up and the shot tighten to mid-body (2 of 4).
- **How a line is said.** Under a line (Day slot, Story scene, and each _Says…_ line in _Make it 30 s_) a **How** row picks _Natural, Whisper, Laughing, Excited, Tender, Teasing_ or _Angry_; it changes how LTX-2.5 delivers the words, in talking clips, conversations (the first line) and 30-second parts. Measured on two stills, same line and seed: all 14 clips still said the words exactly; Whisper came out 6–7 LU quieter and breathy (voiced frames 34–43% vs 73–75%, flat pitch); Excited and Angry raised the pitch from ~254 Hz to 360–410 Hz and read the line with an exclamation; Tender sat low and steady (~190 Hz); Laughing ~320 Hz; Teasing a little quieter and lower. Lip-sync held (0.62–0.75, one teasing clip 0.48 where she turned her head).
- **Make it 30 s parts have sound.** Parts without a line came out as digital silence (LTX took _"nobody speaks"_ literally); they now describe the place's sound — room tone, the small sounds of what is happening — and keep speech out through the negative only.
- **Lines in a 30-second clip.** After _Write the beats_, each part of an LTX clip has an optional _Says…_ line: that part speaks it, lip-synced, while doing its beat (the speech negative is dropped for it). WAN clips (two-person adult clips) cannot move lips to words — the sheet says so and their parts stay wordless.
- **Fix: a voiced clip could turn silent again.** Day's gallery sync put back the URL of the clip's original render, so an _Add voice_ (and now _Make it 30 s_) result could be replaced by the short, silent clip a moment later. A replaced clip now points at its own Gallery entry (the render's job is kept for _Make it 30 s_ to replay), and "the clip changed while…" compares clip jobs, not URLs the sync rewrites.
- **Long and voiced clips are kept in the Gallery.** _Make it 30 s_ and _Add voice_ results used to live only as a ComfyUI input file linked from the slot — not in the Gallery, and lost if ComfyUI's input folder was cleaned. They are now stored like any Gallery clip (kinds _Extend_ and _Voice_, linked to the clip they came from) and the slot points at the kept copy.
- **Make it 30 s survives leaving the page.** The job is remembered on the slot / scene: reload, switch tools or close the tab and it is picked up again when Day or Story opens (if the server restarted meanwhile, the slot says so and stops waiting).
- **Make it 30 s waits its turn.** Its parts queue behind renders already waiting in ComfyUI instead of jumping ahead of them.
- **Direct the 30 seconds.** _Make it 30 s_ opens a sheet: say where the clip should go (_"they finish their coffee, she grabs her bag, kisses him goodbye and heads for the door"_), tap _Write the beats_ for one line per part written from the scene and your direction, edit any line, remove parts or add more (up to 8 for a longer clip), then start — your beats are used as written; with none, they are written for you. Every part now names the slot's setting and rules out close-ups and new people: on a directed goodbye, the kiss had gone to an extreme close-up and the next part rebuilt the kitchen as a café full of strangers; the same six beats again kept the kitchen for all 32 s — bag, kiss in a wide shot, walk to the door, out.
- **Make it 30 s.** A finished Day or Story clip has _Make it 30 s_ (card menu): the LLM writes what happens next from the scene (a few small beats, the same people and place, at the scene's heat), and the clip is carried on in parts that continue from each other, then joined into one MP4 of about 30 seconds that replaces the clip (the short one stays in the Gallery). LTX-2.5 clips continue from their last 17 frames — the motion carries over and the overlap is crossfaded (spike: framing and clothes held where last-frame chaining zoomed in and changed her clothes by 15 s; seam jumps 2.2–3.4× → 1.0–2.2× normal motion) — with LTX's room sound and the face pass. WAN clips (two-person adult clips) replay their own ComfyUI graph from the last frame, so the engine, LoRAs and settings stay the clip's. Each start frame and segment is colour-matched to the clip's first frame (a 7-hop WAN chain had turned the room pink by 21 s; drift 26–36 → 4–9), and WAN segments are told to keep the camera still (it pushed in every hop). Live: a 5 s LTX kitchen clip → 31 s in about 6 minutes; a 4 s WAN diner clip → 29.5 s in about 15 minutes; no seam among the biggest frame jumps. Known limits: LTX framing can still drift in (1 of 2 runs), and faces and clothes can drift late in the clip.
- **One Engine editor everywhere.** Every page's Engine chip now opens the same Engine sheet Outfit and Story use, on desktop as well as phones; the docked Engine column beside Day, Refine, Compose and the other tools is gone, so each page keeps its full width.
- **Suggested lines fit the scene.** _Suggest a line_ asks for four candidate lines (a question, an invitation, a reaction, a joke — or for adult heat a request, a reaction, praise) said to the partner in the scene or to whoever is filming, about one concrete thing there, and keeps the one that names the most of the scene, skipping stock words ("perfect light"), the "That X? …" formula and lines already used in the Day. It no longer narrates the action ("Clinking cups with you"), and a Day slot uses the beat its still was made from. Day speaks with the Day Cast's personality rather than the Story persona's, and the catchphrase is left out (it put coffee into a beach bar). Story's writer gets the same "about something in the scene" rule. On 8 vacation beats: "That straw's stuck halfway, want me to nudge it?", "Your knee's higher than mine, no wonder you're winning this step", "That chocolate's melting right at the edge" instead of "Sorry, I didn't mean to wake the dawn with my elbow".
- **Setup lists everything sound needs.** The five LTX-2.5 files behind talking clips, conversations and Add voice are now downloads under Settings → ComfyUI → Models, marked _needs HF_TOKEN_ (Lightricks gates the repo: accept its terms once). Play checks has a _Score this film (ACE-Step 1.5)_ row saying whether the nodes and model are there. The Play guide lists what each sound feature needs.
- **Conversations: two people talking in one shot.** On a two-person clothed still, a slot or scene with a line can also have _Their reply_: Animate makes one clip where the lead says the line and the other person answers, each with their own voice and their own lip movement (LTX-2.5, about 6 s, full frame at 1152 px, the face pass on). _Suggest a reply_ asks the LLM for the other person's answer to the line. A kept Cast voice is left out of conversations (it would pull both voices toward hers). Live: both lines spoken in order word for word, two voices (her line ~230–280 Hz, his reply ~145–180 Hz), and each speaker's face moving during their own line. Two-person adult clips stay on WAN without speech.
- **Score this film.** Cut options on Day and Story have _Score this film_: an original instrumental in the film's mood, sized to the cut, made in ComfyUI with ACE-Step 1.5 (core ComfyUI nodes; the model is _ACE-Step 1.5 (film scores)_ under Settings → ComfyUI → Models, 10 GB). The Day mood or theme picks the style — Workday upbeat indie pop, Intimate slow sensual downtempo, Night out nu-disco, Lazy Sunday lo-fi — and a Story its tone (noir jazz, romantic piano, epic orchestral…). It becomes the cut's music: fit-to-music, cuts on the beat and turning the music down under spoken lines all apply. Live: 30 s of music in about 8 s; a Workday cut with two talking clips kept both lines clear over it.
- **Talking clips get a face pass.** LTX-2.5's fast model still smeared her mouth in some frames while she talked (user: "still a little facial distortion"). When ComfyUI has ReActor with CodeFormer (or GFPGAN), each talking clip's frames go through it at visibility 0.6 / fidelity 0.7 before the sound is added. Replaying the user's clip on its seed: the smeared and blurred frames came out sharp, same face, no added flicker (frame-to-frame jitter 0.893 → 0.883), lip-sync unchanged. More refine steps, no start-frame compression or no voice did not help. Without ReActor the clip is unchanged — Settings → Overview → Play checks now has a _Talking-clip face pass (ReActor)_ row: **Install** puts the ReActor pack on through ComfyUI-Manager, and Settings → ComfyUI → Models has _CodeFormer face restore_ when ReActor has no restore model (a new _Face restore_ model kind, `models/facerestore_models`).
- **Full-frame talking clips render larger.** With _Keep the full frame_ (or a still that isn't cropped) the face was about 60 px in a 768-px clip and the moving mouth smeared (user: "back to getting the face distortion"). Those clips now render with a 1152-px long side: replaying the user's clip on its seed, the face and mouth stayed clean (about 63 s instead of 45 s). Chest-up clips stay at 768.
- **Keep the full frame.** A Day slot or Story scene with a line has a _Keep the full frame_ switch: its talking clip animates the whole still instead of starting chest-up when her face is small. For cuts where the wide framing matters more than readable lips.
- **A kept Cast voice no longer warps the face.** The voice LoRA (trained on LTX-2.3) now steers only the first, half-size pass, at 0.5; the refine pass that draws the final face runs on the plain model with the first pass's audio frozen. Replaying the user's two clips on their own seeds: the LoRA on both passes warped the face (smeared mouth; a white-mask face as she walked into the camera); first pass only at 0.5 was clean on both, like no voice at all. Voice match to the kept sample: 0.79 on one clip (0.65 without the voice), no change on the other — so steering stays a switch, off by default.

## [v2.4.3] - 2026-10-10

- **Talking clips no longer warp her face when a Cast voice is kept.** With a kept voice, talking clips ran the LTX ID-LoRA at full strength; it was trained on LTX-2.3, and on LTX-2.5 it smeared and warped the face around the mouth (user: "weird artifacts around her mouth and face"). Replaying the user's own clip on its seed: with the LoRA the face warped in every sampled frame; without it, clean; at half strength, mostly clean. Steering clips toward the kept voice is now a switch on Cast → Voice (_Steer talking clips toward this voice_), off by default, and runs the LoRA at 0.6. Kept voices stay saved.

## [v2.4.2] - 2026-10-10

- **Talking clips sync: closer framing, and she stays to say the line.** From a full-body still the face was about 20 px tall, so the spoken line was there but the lips couldn't be read (user: night slot "not syncing"); and the clip still animated the beat ("walking out of the office building… mid-stride", "no wide-open mouth"), so she turned and walked out of frame mid-line. A talking clip now starts from a chest-up crop around the face when the face is small in the still (the app's face locator; same pixels, so the face and outfit stay hers), and its prompt has her stop, face the camera and say the line with small movements only. Live, same still / line / seeds: mouth-motion vs loudness 0.68 / 0.73 cropped vs 0.64 / 0.54 full-frame; the talking prompt 0.57 / 0.69 vs 0.53 / 0.59 with her staying in frame. A clip with an end pose keeps the full still. Clips made before this keep their framing — Animate again.

## [v2.4.1] - 2026-10-10

- **Add voice no longer puts words in a clip it cannot lip-sync.** On two-person adult clips (rendered on WAN, silent) _Add voice_ spoke the slot's line over the unchanged picture, so the words never matched the mouth (user: "talking sync seems to have broken"). Measured as mouth-motion vs loudness: 0.34–0.52 on those clips against 0.55–0.75 on LTX's own talking clips; re-rendering the face lightly to follow the audio did not raise it, and a stronger pass changed the scene. _Add voice_ now gives them sound without words — breathing, moans, the room (no speech detected on live dubs, where the old ones spoke the line) — brought to a steady level (the wordless sound came out near −50 dB), and a line is spoken only on clips LTX renders itself. Talking clips from wide stills (a small face, head turned away) sync less well: the line field says so; a camera push-in synced the lips but changed her face, so it isn't used.

## [v2.4.0] - 2026-10-10

- **Talking clips.** Give a Day slot or a Story scene a _Line in the clip_ and Animate makes a talking clip: the Cast says the line to the camera, lip-synced, with sound (LTX-2.5, about 5 s, saved as MP4). LTX-2.5 always generated a soundtrack with the picture; Castcut now keeps it. A line moves that clip onto LTX-2.5 even when WAN is the clip engine (only LTX makes sound). Live: 13 of 13 clips said their line word for word (speech-to-text), 40–45 s each.
- **Suggested lines.** _Suggest a line_ on the slot / scene sheet asks the LLM for one line from the beat, the time of day and the Cast's personality, and the Story writer suggests a line with every scene (_Use "…"_ on the field). Nothing talks until you put a line in. The tone follows the Day mood or Story rating: clean on Everyday / Sport / Vacation, teasing on Suggestive, a murmur to a lover on Intimate, short breathless dirty talk on Raunchy (explicit words allowed, consenting adults only).
- **Voice for two-person scenes (_Add voice_).** Two-person adult clips stay on WAN (LTX-2.5 drifts off the act), and WAN makes no sound. _Add voice_ (a slot's or scene's ⋯ menu) gives a finished silent clip a soundtrack without touching the picture: the clip is frozen and LTX-2.5 writes only its audio — the line if there is one, else breathing and moans. A slot or scene with a line gets it automatically when its silent clip lands. Live: a WAN straddle clip voiced in 39 s, the line heard word for word.
- **Cast voices.** Cast → Bible → _Voice_: hear the Cast in four kinds of voice (_Audition voices_ renders four short clips of them saying hello), keep one, make it a little deeper or higher. Later talking clips are steered toward the kept voice with the LTX ID-LoRA (Settings → ComfyUI → Models → _LTX ID-LoRA (Cast voices)_, 1.1 GB, LTX-2 Community License). Same still, line and seeds: similarity to her voice 0.81 / 0.78 vs 0.74 / 0.69 without a sample, and the pitch locks to hers. It steers the voice; it does not clone it. _Use this voice_ on any talking clip keeps that clip's voice instead.
- **Lines in the cut.** Cut film keeps each talking clip's sound at its place in the film, holds a talking clip for its whole line, and turns the music down under the speech.
- Clips with sound play in the lightbox (gallery clips have no file extension in their address, so a talking MP4 opened as a blank picture) with sound on, and Story's reel cards have controls on talking clips.
- **Places: one home, one workplace.** Every Day still set in a bedroom, kitchen, living room, bathroom, hallway or balcony was a different apartment, and every office a different office. Each Cast now has a home and a workplace — a fixed look for the whole place (walls, floors, windows) plus each room's own pieces — added to every still set there. Every Cast gets one without setting anything up; Cast → Bible → _Places_ picks another (4 homes, 3 offices), takes your own words, or turns it off. Story's writer gets the same place. Live (Rapid, 8 home beats): off, a different home in every still; with a place, one apartment throughout.
- **Day theme: Workday.** Dressing for work, the train, the office desk, presenting at a whiteboard, the office kitchen, leaving at golden hour, a glass of wine after work, heels off at home, the last email from the couch; with companions on, a friend from work in the lift, at one desk, at lunch, for after-work drinks and on the walk to the train. Dresses from business casual outfits, trouser and two- or three-piece suits, shirt and sheath dresses and blazers. Built on Everyday like the other themes; every scene has its own room and a stance the pose system knows.
- **Suggestive is more risqué: implied nudity and bolder boudoir.** New Day beats — solo (a sheet held across her chest, a towel after a bath, bare back on the bed, only an unbuttoned shirt held closed; lace bodysuits, stockings and garters, all fours on the bed, straddling a chair) and couple (under the sheets, one sheet or towel for two, a bubble bath, him unhooking her bra). An implied beat takes its own coverage line (`clothed-coverage.ts`: chest covered by an arm, hands, a sheet or a towel; never a nipple, genitals or a bare bottom) instead of "her clothes stay on", for one woman, a couple, two women or two men. The adult check's "bare" question counts an arm, hands, hair, a sheet or a towel over the chest as covered, and still re-renders a still that shows more. Story's Suggestive rating invites lingerie and implied nudity with the same limits.
- Live, three rounds (Rapid, Edit 2511 and Qwen-Image 2.1 solo; Rapid and Edit 2511 couples — 69 stills, no nipple or genitals shown). Fixes from them: implied scenes skip the dress plate and the picked shoes (a kit's boots ended up on her lying on the bed); the side-lying sheet beat covers her hips too; the open-shirt beats grip the shirt's own edges (every engine drew a separate cloth for "holding it closed"); an implied couple says what each has on — her sheet, towel or bath, him bare-chested — instead of "a flirty dress / shirt and jeans" (he sat in the bubble bath in jeans). Open-shirt beats kept drawing a separate towel in her hands: the coverage line offered "a sheet or a towel". An implied beat's line now names only the cover it has — the shirt itself (hands empty), the sheet, the towel or bath foam: shirt beats 4 of 4 without a towel, replayed on the same graphs.
- **Known limits.** Places don't reach the Intimate / Raunchy moods yet (their recipes pick and write their own rooms). Talking-clip sound is kept by the server's Cut film encoder; the in-browser fallback encoder still drops it. A kept voice steers talking clips toward it but isn't a clone, and the local LLM's suggested lines can repeat an opening — _Another line_ rolls again.
- **Play / shared boundary guard (step 1 of splitting out classic Prompt Studio).** A unit test builds the import graph and fails on any new import into the Play layer (Film, Cast, Look, Day, Outfit, Story) from shared or Studio code. Today's 152 crossings are recorded in `architecture/play-boundary-baseline.json`; the test also fails when one disappears, so the list only shrinks. The plan, the layers and the largest crossings (settings-cache, the mobile shell, studio-extras) are in `docs/architecture-boundaries.md`.
- Step 2 so far: Cast-change clean-up and the Day face-restore follow-up now register from Play (`registerCastChangeScrubber`, `registerJobCompletedHook`, wired by `PlayFeatures` from the root layout).
