Three shared helpers lost misleading Play names: `skin-refine`, `plate-render-size` and `prompt-recipe-mark`. Crossings went from 152 to 138, in 77 files instead of 89.
- UI slots and flags (`AppSlot`, `AppSlotOwner`, `app-flags`). The home dashboard's film cards, the queue's continue chip and the Gallery empty state (now `play/PlayGalleryEmptyPanel`, with a plain fallback for the classic app) are registered by Play. Files used only by Play are listed in `architecture/play-owned.json`. Crossings: 82, in 39 files.
- Data hooks: Play's synced fields (play metrics, campaign, saved clothing photos, dress plates) moved into a studio-extras section, with the same keys and apply rules. Play now registers its own local-data reset and the empty-screen "resume your film" link. The first-film onboarding milestones moved into `play-onboarding`. Crossings: 69, in 35 files.
- Pose data shapes moved to shared `pose-types`. The Gallery pose dialog lists registered pose targets (Play: Day slots, Story beats). The Queue page's Day/Story job labels and "Run next" repointing are a registered describer. The render core no longer imports Play at all. Crossings: 60, in 29 files.
- Play's tool settings types and defaults moved to `play-settings`, joined to the shared settings by TypeScript declaration merging. The phone app (`/m`) is classed as Castcut's. Crossings: 38, in 25 files; type-only ones went from 29 to 8.
- The Cast store split. `character-os` keeps records, looks, identity and the bible picture (now the shared `character-bible-picture`). Story fields, film cuts, Look packs and Cast-from-Story moved to `play-cast`, and a test checks feature fields survive normalising without Play. Crossings: 33, in 24 files.
- Shared media and plate helpers. Video and film checks moved to `media-kind`, and a look's plate resolvers to `character-plate` (Story's, Cast's and Outfit's modules re-export them). `cast-plate-thumb` became the shared `character-plate-thumb`. The Cast-plate watcher is mounted by `PlayFeatures` instead of the app shell. Crossings: 22, in 16 files.
- Last shared-to-Play imports. Cast identity on queued jobs moved to `character-identity`, face locating to `face-locate-client`, and beat reading to `beat-text`. Nav links resolve through `nav-links`, Cast pickers through `character-hooks`, and Gallery keepers through `gallery-judgment-hooks`; Play registers its behaviour for each. The first-run welcome and Settings' Play checks render through app slots (`shell.welcome`, `settings.firstRunChecks`). Clip assembly is now shared: `video-assemble` (Gallery stitch, Video continue), with the server encoder renamed from `film-*` to `video-*`. Play keeps the film cut (`character-film-assemble`). Crossings: 1 (the app shell's kiosk mode, which is step 3).
- Shell split (step 3). `AppShell` takes the Film kiosk from the root layout as a prop, and Play's bundle hoists moved to `PlayFeatures`. The guard now also reads bare `import 'x';` lines; it found the two hoists, which are fixed by this change. Crossings: 0.
- Classic Prompt Studio app (step 4a). `apps/prompt-studio` is a second Next app on the shared code, with generated route wrappers (`npm run gen:classic`), its own layout without Play, and `NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_PROFILE=classic` (name, workspaces, nav). Castcut's root layout became the shared `RootDocument`, and its Next config moved to `next.config.base.cjs`. The classic type-check found shared code using Play's merged types; fixed. Story bible helpers moved to `play-cast`, Play's Cast fields are kept through `registerStickyCharacterFields`, and Workflow health and LoRA "Check on Cast" take Day's partner and plate through registrations. CI builds the classic app.
- Castcut trim (step 3). The plugin runtime and the adult generator plugin load only in Prompt Studio (`PromptStudioFeatures` in its layouts); the scheduled scene batch and scheduled campaign, which run Generate, are Prompt Studio's (hidden in Castcut's Settings, runner not mounted); the admin feature picker lists only features whose pages the app has (`featureInApp`). Simple and Full are already folded into Film and Studio in Castcut and stay real modes in Prompt Studio.
- Castcut without the classic tools (step 2). Prompt Studio's own pages moved to `src/studio-app` (not routed by Castcut): Generate, Prompt Editor, Image → Prompt, Variations, Outpaint, ControlNet, Format, Lint, Negative, Topics, Audio, Mesh, Logo, Fantasy, Pet, Background, Studio, the workflow editor and Plugins. Castcut keeps Video, Compose, Refine, Inpaint and the Dashboard; links to the moved pages are hidden (`appHasRoute`, `AppRouteLink`). Prompt Studio's routes come from both folders. Their e2e tests are tagged `@classic` and run against the classic app (`npm run test:e2e:classic`).
- Film-only Castcut (step 1). Castcut's "/" opens Film; its sidebar, command palette and Gallery handoffs list only Castcut's pages: Film, Cast, Look, Outfit, Day, Story, Character, Video, Refine, Inpaint, Compose, Gallery and Queue (`appOffersRoute`). The other classic tools are Prompt Studio's; their pages still answer by URL for now. Workspaces are Film and Studio (Simple becomes Film, Full becomes Studio); Film's "All tools" opens the sidebar layout on the Gallery.
- Prompt Studio wording. Castcut's product name, film-loop copy and Play links no longer show in the classic app. `appHasRoute` drops links to Play pages (nav, scene switcher, onboarding steps); the output prefix, ComfyUI workflow folder, login, TOTP issuer and bug-report repo follow the app; Dashboard, Queue, Video, Settings About and the Data funnel show Castcut's film copy only in Castcut. Castcut's own wording is unchanged.
- **Side-lying solo looks natural.** "Top knee raised high" shot the top leg straight up in a near-split: 4 of 7 of the user's own renders of "alone on her side… at 3 a.m.", the most re-rolled beat since 2026-10-06 (six sessions), and 3 of 3 replays. The recipe now has the knee drawn forward with the foot resting on the mattress: natural, hand at her vulva, 3 of 3.
- **Solo fingering: fingers inside, not two hands framing.** Seated and on-her-back solo stills whose beat says "both hands" came out with both hands spreading her open and the fingers beside the vulva, about 11 of 12. The recipe now puts two fingers of one hand inside, palm against her vulva, and the other hand on her breast, with the Moment worded the same way: fingering 10 of 12. On her back with knees pulled to her chest (or ankles near her shoulders), one arm now hooks under a knee to hold that leg back while the other hand fingers: about 6 of 8, against about 1 of 8 before. Toy beats are unchanged.
- **Raunchy clothing gags stay nude.** Moments like "as her nightshirt rides up" or "pulls her shorts down" kept the garment on a nude still (a white top 2 of 2, shorts 1 of 2). The nude recipe now drops the garment line ("pulls her close"): nude 6 of 6. This applies to every pairing.
- **Two-women lap.** "Side view of the chair… sits in it" sat them side by side in all 12 sweep and replay renders. The two-men lap wording ("sits on top of her, astride… one on the other's lap", side view) puts one on the other's lap 5 of 6. Also checked from the sweep: a man appearing in two-women stills (3 of 86) is seed luck, not the pose map. No man in 24 replays with or without the map, and removing the map made the framing worse.
- **Two-men Raunchy: cowgirl and picked-up lifts.** "Cowgirl" in a two-men Moment gave a man a vulva (1 of 2). It now reads "riding his boyfriend" ("…facing away" for reverse), with male anatomy 4 of 4. "Picked up and fucked by…" lifts get the counter-edge wording: seated 4 of 4.
- **Gallery backups setting.** Settings → Data → Gallery backups → "Keep a backup copy of each render" is on by default: the Gallery copies every finished still and clip so renders survive ComfyUI purging its outputs. Turn it off to save disk space; new renders then live only in ComfyUI's output folder. Copies already kept stay.
- **Progress shows steps ComfyUI reused.** When ComfyUI skips steps it has cached (the second of Two takes, a re-roll), the progress line adds "· N steps reused".
- **Two-men lift.** "Lifted onto his boyfriend… legs wrapped" came out as a standing hug (4 of 4). The recipe now seats him on the counter edge with his boyfriend standing between his knees, and the Moment says the same: 5 of 6.
- **Duo still counts skip the queue (castcut-nodes 1.6.0).** The two-person count check (faces, hands, bodies, wrists, ankles and parts, used to order Two takes) ran as a queued graph and waited behind whatever was rendering (31 s seen behind a sweep still). The pack's new `analyze` `duo-counts` op runs the same Impact detector nodes in-process on the CPU, plus DWPose, in about 1 s. It matched the queued graph on 8 of 8 live stills, and the graph remains the fallback. The LoRA check can't move: it re-renders the still, which has to queue; its face scoring already uses the pack.
- **Two-men adult stills: clothes, lap and straddle.** From the same-sex sweep, tested on its own graphs over three seeds:
- **Clothes:** "a dress pushed up" becomes "a shirt and trousers pushed up" for two men, and that slipped past the nude recipe's clothes filter, dressing the partner (4 of 4). Now both are nude, 6 of 6.
- **Lap:** they sat side by side (4 of 4). The two-women wording, astride his lap as "two separate bodies", in side view: 6 of 6.
- **Straddle on the bed:** the rider stood over him (2 of 2). Now he sits upright astride him, knees on the mattress: 3 of 3.
- **Not fixed:** lift beats still miss.
- **Outfit and dress plates are back on Edit 2511.** Klein 9B Distilled was faster but lost the picked clothing's detail. Outfit's default engine is Edit 2511 again: an Outfit switched to Klein goes back once, and Klein stays available in the Engine chip. Dress plates (Day, Story, Outfit Keep) render on Edit 2511 when it's installed. Plates now record the engine that made them; Klein-made plates, including unmarked ones from 2026-10-08 when Klein was the default, are re-made on 2511 the next time they're needed.
- **Man–woman oral contact on Rapid.** When she goes down on him, the recipe said "both faces in frame… looking up at him", and she knelt beside him holding it with her mouth off it (0 of 6). It now uses the side view that fixed two men: her face in profile at his hips or lap, "exactly one man and one woman". Contact went to 5 of 6 on the user's sweep graphs; one render added a third person.
- **Story points at the right pose map.** With no clothing image, a Story still's pose map goes in as Image 2, but the prompt called it "Image 3", a picture that wasn't there (101 of the user's Story stills). Story now renumbers the way Day does. The adult nude line ("Image 1 and Image 2 fabric…") no longer blocks the renumbering on Day or Story. A same-seed replay of 10 stills changed little: Rapid follows the map in its slot either way. This is a correctness fix.
- **No limit on saved clothing photos and shoes.** Both lists kept the newest 12 and silently dropped older ones; now they keep everything. The picker strip scrolls, and Browse searches the whole list. Pack imports no longer warn about overflow.
- **Outfit stage keeps its place while rendering.** The try-on renders in a fixed square frame with ComfyUI's live preview and the queue position / render % (as on Day). With Front and back on, the back frame is there from the start and fills when the back view lands, so the page no longer jumps. Picking an earlier try-on from the strip holds until the next render starts.
- **Outfit keeps every try-on.** The stage strip was capped at 4 (the oldest dropped off); now it keeps them all.
- **Outfit back view keeps the shoes.** The back view now names the picked shoes. For heels it adds a keep-the-heel-shape line: stilettos had come back as block heels 4 of 4 times, 0 of 4 with the line. For flat shoes (sneakers, flats, loafers…) it says flat soles, no heel; this follows a report of sneakers turning into kitten heels.
- **Wardrobe packs: export and import your clothing and shoes.** Under Saved photos and Saved shoes in the Clothing picker (Outfit, Day, Story): **Export pack** downloads your saved clothing photos or saved shoes as a .zip (the packshots plus their descriptions and shoe words); **Import pack** adds a pack's clothing and/or shoes to your saved lists, uploading the pictures to ComfyUI and skipping ones you already have. For moving to another install or sharing. Checked in a browser: 12 clothing photos exported, re-import said all 12 were already saved, a fresh install added all 12; a two-pair shoe pack added both.
- **Day's camera moves.** Every Day still was the same shot — full body, eye level. Clothed one-person stills on Edit 2511 now rotate by time slot through eye level, a low angle, a slightly high angle and a wide shot with more of the place, whole body always in frame (a re-take keeps its slot's camera). Edit 2511 followed all three on four of your Everyday stills with the pose, outfit and shoes kept. Duos, Sport and the adult moods are unchanged.
- **Walking hand in hand holds hands.** The stride line said "arms swinging", which fought the hold: hands were joined in about 4 of 8 takes. It now says their near hands are joined, fingers interlaced: 8 of 8 (four hand-in-hand beats, two seeds).
- **Shoes on auto follow the beat.** "Lacing boots" got white sneakers from this morning's automatic pick; a beat that names boots, heels, sandals, sneakers or loafers now gets that kind. "Walking home" no longer counts as a bare-feet place ("at home" still does).
- **Scissoring is scissoring again.** Since Oct 5 a scissoring beat rendered as missionary (the seated wording and its pose drawing merged legs and once added a third person). Without the drawing and with each leg placed in words, a couple scissors for real: 5–6 of 6 across a couch and a bed, three seeds each. Two women keep the side-by-side pose that tested clean; two men keep kneeling.
- **No mirror twin on the bathroom-sink solo.** "Leaning back against the mirror" and "fogged mirror" drew her reflection as a second figure on every take (3/3); she now leans on both hands on the counter in front of a plain tiled wall (0/3).
- **A pose drawing that contradicts the scene no longer judges it.** The sink beat's drawing is a standing figure, so the pose check called the (correct) sitting still a miss and the redo asked for "body standing". When the drawing's posture disagrees with the posture the scene words give, the pose check stands aside.
- **Klein Enhancer settings never reached ComfyUI.** The settings sent with each queue were trimmed of every Klein Enhancer field (on/off, text enhancer, color anchor and its strength, identity preset), so the server always used the pack's defaults (anchor 0.45) whatever Settings said — and Outfit's 0.1 cap never applied. They are kept now.
- **Outfit try-ons on white, in even light.** A Look handed Outfit its whole moodboard prompt ("Compose a cohesive scene…", sunset backlight, rooftop, amber palette) as styling notes, so a try-on asked for white seamless and a sunset rooftop at once — warm, hard-lit, over-baked Klein try-ons on rooftops. The try-on now drops those scene lines (already-saved notes too), asks for soft, even studio light on white, and a Look gives Outfit only its style notes. Re-rendered on two of your try-ons, both seeds: white background, even light, same outfit and shoes.
- **Fix: opening Outfit in a new browser could wipe its settings on the server.** The automatic switch to Klein (added earlier today) saved Outfit's settings while the first pull from the server was still running; edits made then win per tool, so the near-empty local Outfit record replaced the server's (plate, clothing photo, shoes). The switch now waits until this page has synced with the server. Checked with a fresh browser on a copy of the data: everything kept, engine switched.
- **Day's dress plate renders on Klein 9B Distilled when it is installed.** Tested on Lana in her burgundy lace-up dress and woven wedges, then six of her own Day stills started from each plate: the Klein plate took 10–24 s against about 80 s on Edit 2511, came out with the wedges on (the 2511 plates were barefoot on both seeds and needed a shoe pass), and the Day stills built on it were as close to her face (0.51 vs 0.59, lower is closer). It renders portrait like the Cast plates, with the color anchor at 0.1. Without Klein, plates render on the still's engine as before.
- **One dress plate for every engine.** Plates were filed by the engine that asked for them, so an Outfit Keep made on Klein was never found by a Day on Edit 2511. A plate is now keyed by the Cast plate, clothing and shoes only (stored plates are dressed once more under the new key).
- **Klein Outfit try-ons no longer look over-baked.** The Klein Enhancer's color anchor (0.45 by default) gave Klein 9B Distilled try-ons hard contrast, darker crunchy skin and over-sharp hair. At 0.1 they read as photos and keep their colours; switching the text enhancer off made no visible difference (A/B on two of your try-ons, 2 seeds). Outfit caps the anchor at 0.1; the Settings value is unchanged for everything else. The Klein Enhancer pack is current (3.4.4).
- **Story is reel first.** The page opened on setup (the identity photo card with Choose File / Isolate / Scan, the outfit row, an Engine column) with the story itself further down. Now the story leads: the newest scene large with all its actions, the scenes so far as a numbered strip (tap one to bring it up; a failed or flagged scene is marked), Cut film under it, then What happens next. Cast, photo and outfit sit in a slim side column (the Cast card folds once it is set up), with Animate → Cut beside the story. The Engine opens from the chip. Gone: the second step bar, the Watch player as a second big picture (now "Watch the story so far"), and the empty reel's extra Roll button. Phone Story uses the same stage near the top and hides the Character name box when a Cast lead names the story.
- **Story used another Cast's photo.** Story only loaded the Cast's plate when it had no photo, so a photo set for another Cast stayed: continuing as Loose Lana, Story still held Tomas's cutout and would start stills from it whenever no dress plate or face crop took over. Story's photo now remembers whose it is and reloads for the active Cast. The phone's Story card showed the old phone plate list (Tomas) instead of the photo beats queue with; it shows Story's own photo and the Cast's name now.
- **"Is it installed?" was always unknown with the server's ComfyUI address.** With no ComfyUI URL set in the browser, the cached inventory was never found, so checks that pick an engine by what is installed (the shoe pass's Edit 2511, Outfit's default) saw nothing. They now read the server-default inventory, and Outfit fetches it when nothing is cached.
- **Outfit on Klein 9B Distilled.** Tested on 8 of your saved clothing photos, two seeds each, against Edit 2511: Klein was about three times faster (median 16 s vs 55 s), put the picked shoes on 12 of 12 try-ons (2511: 2 of 12 before the shoe pass) and kept her face nearly as close to the plate. Outfit now switches to it by itself when it is installed (else to Edit 2511), once — pick another engine and that stays, until a better default is installed. With Klein the shoe pass no longer runs blind when the shoe check can't answer (it would swap to Edit 2511). A clothing photo of a top alone left her in the plate's underwear on Klein; the try-on now asks for simple bottoms when Image 2 is only a top (3 of 3 with shorts; dresses, sets and bodysuits unchanged).
- **Outfit is a fitting room.** The page was a long column of setup cards (Character, Plate, Pose, Clothing behind a Choose… sheet, Quality, a row of buttons) with an Engine column beside it and the try-ons at the bottom. On desktop it is now three columns: who is trying on and her plate · the try-on itself, large, with Keep / Again / Fix / Pass and earlier try-ons as a strip under it · the clothes picker right on the page, with Try it on at the top of that column (it stays in reach under the header as you scroll the kits and shoes). The Engine, quality by hand, LoRA and identity lock open from the Engine chip. The page's own second step bar, the "Ready to keep" note and "Skip outfit · Day" are gone (Continue to Day is in the header). The phone page leads with the same stage under a "Trying on" row, with plate controls folded away once there are try-ons.
- **Outfit tried clothes on the wrong person after a Cast switch.** Picking another Cast kept the previous Cast's plate (Loose Lana picked, Tomas's plate and face used). The plate now remembers whose it is and reloads when the Cast changes.
- **Picked shoes on Outfit try-ons, even when the shoe check can't run.** The fitting page checks her feet after a try-on and puts the picked shoes on when they're missing. When the check couldn't run — most often LM Studio failing to load its vision model while ComfyUI is busy — the shoe pass was skipped, and plain try-ons with picked shoes come out right only about 1 time in 10 (Castcut_03148 stayed barefoot that way). The pass now runs whenever shoes were picked and the check gave no answer; the same for Day's dress plate.
- **No more bare feet in the street.** With shoes left on auto, the short prompts every Day still now uses named no shoes and the Cast plate is barefoot, so she walked city streets, boardwalks, subway platforms and crosswalks barefoot (the overnight sweep of 513 clothed stills: across Everyday, Vacation and the themes; Sport kits were fine). Day now names a pair that suits the outfit and the place — sneakers, loafers with a suit, heeled sandals with an evening dress (never for a man), flat sandals on vacation: 8 of 8 in replays. Bare feet stay at home, in bed, at the beach or pool, on a yoga or martial-arts mat, in a robe or swimwear; shoes you picked always win.
- **Face finish no longer roughens her skin.** The face pass asked for "natural skin texture, sharp natural detail" with the `euler` sampler, and often added freckles, red spots and an older, blotchy look. It now asks for clear, even skin and samples with `euler_ancestral`: clean on 4 of 4 replays of your stills, with likeness slightly closer. It also runs on the fp8 Edit 2511 files when installed, like Day stills, with the same faces.

## [v2.3.5] - 2026-10-07

- **Smaller app bundle.** The gallery's "change the outfit" variant loaded the whole wardrobe catalog (~500 KB gzipped, the app's largest chunk) into the browser to pick one outfit. The pick now runs on the server (`/api/catalog/wardrobe-mutation`); a named outfit needs no catalog at all. Client JS went from 3.37 MB to 2.88 MB gzipped.
- **Two women and two men, tested.** Today's fixes were re-run for same-sex pairs. Face-down and spooning already worked. Two women's lap turned her to the camera with her girlfriend behind (2 of 2); it's now a side view, face to face (3 of 3). Two men against a wall posed apart; they now drop the pose drawing and get the side view (in contact, 2 of 2). Two women against a wall were already in contact with the drawing, so it stays.
- **Standing sex against a wall, window or fridge now shows sex, not a pose.** An overnight sweep of every Intimate and Raunchy beat (132 beats, 2 seeds) found these came out as the couple standing side by side in 8 of 12 beats. The pose drawing had two people standing straight; without it, and with a side-view description (no gap between their bodies), 4 of 4 were in contact. Face-down sex with a partner also drops its drawing: 4 of 4 face-down, up from 1 of 4. Two-women and two-men wall stills keep the drawing until they're tested.
- **Day stills render about 1.8× faster on Edit 2511** when ComfyUI has the fp8 files: `qwen_image_edit_2511_fp8mixed` (Comfy-Org) and the `qwen_2.5_vl_7b_fp8_scaled` text encoder. The bf16 model and encoder (57 GB together) don't fit a 24 GB card and were swapped in and out on every still. On a 4090 the same stills went from 45.8 s to 25.9 s (22.4 s with LM Studio's model unloaded), with the same outfits, faces and skin. Without those files nothing changes.
- **Story opens once a Day's stills are done.** It used to stay locked until you cut your first film. Now it opens as soon as every slot of a Day has its still, cut or not — the Story tab appears on the Play and phone menus, and _Continue as a story_ / _Open with today's Day_ carry the Day into it.
- **Open in ComfyUI opens the still's graph on the canvas.** It used to save the graph and open ComfyUI on whatever it last showed, leaving you to find the file in the Workflows sidebar. With Castcut nodes 1.5.0 installed as a folder (Registry, git or Manager; restart ComfyUI once), the link now loads the graph directly. Otherwise it still saves to `workflows/Castcut/` and says what to install. Opened from another device, a `127.0.0.1` ComfyUI link now uses the host you reached Castcut on.

## [v2.3.4] - 2026-10-07

- **Changing the outfit re-dresses the plate.** When a look's plate was a kept Outfit try-on (a Keep), Day kept starting every Everyday still from it even after you picked a different outfit — the old outfit in the picture, the new one in the words, and stills that mixed the two (a new top over the kept shorts). A Keep in a different clothing photo, kit or shoes than the one picked now is no longer used: Day dresses the Cast's own plate in the new outfit, once, and starts the stills from that. (A first version of this fix started the stills from the undressed Cast plate instead of the dressed one, so each still made up its own dress and shoes; fixed.)
- **Keep a Day partner as Cast.** The invented partner keeps one face from Day to Day (and through Tomorrow); **Keep as Cast** beside that face now makes them a Cast member — their face and look become the Cast, Day switches its partner to them, and they can lead a Day of their own (Setup → _Make a plate from the description_ gives them a body plate).
- **Seasons on the Cast page.** Every cut Day is an episode of the Cast's season; the Cast's Film tab (where _Watch on Cast_ lands) now shows each season's episodes in order — the still it opened on, the places it went, its theme and date — with a count of places visited. Episodes cut from now on carry the poster and places.
- **Face-down sex stays face-down.** "Lying face-down … with a partner along her back" came out with her on her back in 2 of 3 replays (the side-on pose drawing can't show which way she faces); naming her back and buttocks up and her breasts in the sheets fixed it, 3 of 3.
- **Lap and spooning stills fixed** (3 of 3 each, from 0 of 3): sitting on his lap facing him is now drawn from the side with both faces in profile — front-on she turned to the camera and he vanished under her; spooning draws her top knee forward instead of a leg pointing straight at the ceiling.
- **Story can open with your Day.** When a Cast's story is empty and their Day has finished stills, Story offers **Open with today's Day**: each still becomes an opening scene (in Day order, with its picture), the Day's tone, rating, place and outfit come along, and the next scene the writer rolls picks up from what happened. It is a choice on the empty reel, so a story in progress is never replaced.
- **Three more intimate fixes** (each from 2 of 3 bad replays to 0 of 3): a nude solo beat that says _in lingerie_ no longer leaves underwear on; _after dinner_ no longer lays plates of food across sex stills; and standing solo stills no longer sometimes give her a penis — the hand now cups her vulva instead of floating between her thighs.
- **Clothes no longer leak into solo stills on Intimate mixed Days.** The check that a beat is masturbation only matched the bare stem, never _masturbation_ or _masturbating_, so on a mixed (solo + partner) Intimate Day those beats kept the outfit and could come out fully dressed. Fixed in the nude/clothed decision and the other solo-or-partner checks that shared the pattern.
- **Spooning stills untangled.** On Rapid the flat spooning pose drawing fought the words that lift her top leg — legs tangled, or a leg stood up from nowhere. Adult spooning now renders from the words alone: 3 of 3 clean in replays of a tangled still.
- **Another third leg fixed.** Solo stills lying on her back already say where each leg goes (as the pose drawing has them); a beat that also said _knees apart_ drew a third leg in 2 of 3 replays. Those leg words now drop out of the moment line when the stance sets the legs (0 of 3).
- **Tomorrow →** after a cut: the next episode of the Cast's season, written to follow on from the Day you just made — same Cast, mood and people, new places, and one beat that picks up something from yesterday (it also keeps following your _Day from an idea_ line). Kits Day picked by itself change; an outfit you chose stays. Adult moods, or with the LLM off, get a fresh Day that never repeats yesterday's beats. The thread follows each Cast when you switch.
- **Walking hand in hand actually walks.** On Edit 2511 a walking couple was drawn from the standing hold-hands pose map and came out as a posed side-by-side portrait (0 of 3 in replays). Walking beats now skip that map and describe a side-on stride: 3 of 3 walked mid-step, hands joined, both faces in view.
- **Day from an idea.** On an Everyday or Vacation Day (and its themes), type one line — _a rainy Saturday that ends at a gallery opening_ — and **Write the day**: the LLM writes a beat and a place for every slot, morning to night, so the stills follow one thread. Each beat must open with a stance Day can pose; anything adult, in the bath, or with extra people on a solo Day is dropped and that slot keeps its beat.
- **Your take picks are saved with the gallery.** Keeping one of Two takes, or tapping Looks wrong, now marks those stills in the synced gallery, not just this browser — the labelled set needed to build a reliable check for two-person intimate stills.
- **Four intimate stills fixed** (each 3 of 3 in replays, where the old wording failed 3 of 3): face-down on the laundry pile no longer comes out face-up and upside down; _ankles near her shoulders_ raises both legs as the pose guide draws (the one-knee line from the third-leg fix twisted her); the couch beat's TV remote stays on the floor instead of between her legs; and a nude two-person still drops clothes its beat names (_cocktail dress around her waist_, _dress pushed up_) instead of drawing underwear at the waist.
- **Re-rolls are remembered across reloads.** Each take records the beat it was rendered for, so rendering it again after a reload still counts as a re-roll.
- **A Cast plate without Look.** A new Cast made without a photo used to get its first plate when you extracted a look. Now _Create & continue to Day_ opens Day and renders the plate from the character's description on arrival. Day's Setup has _Make a plate from the description_ for any Cast without one. A plate rendered from the description also attaches to the Cast wherever you are. Before, it only attached when Outfit was opened, so a look taken straight to Day left the Cast without a plate.
- **Nude stills no longer keep a white top.** The face crop that stands in for the Cast on nude stills reached down to the shoulders, and a plate in a tank top put that top on the body (5 of 6 replays). The crop now stops just under the collarbones: 6 of 6 nude, likeness about the same.
- **Day learns from your re-rolls.** Rendering the same beat again on a slot is recorded. A layout you re-roll in half its renders or more (8+) counts as a hard pose, so _Best of two for hard poses_ gives it a second take. A beat you've re-rolled 3+ times (at 60%+ of its renders) is skipped by _Suggest day_ while there are others. Auto-review's own redos aren't counted. (Best of two is part of the **Best** quality preset; Balanced leaves it off.)
- **A film is Cast → Day.** Day plans the scenes and dresses your Cast itself, so Look and Outfit are optional steps like Story (_Film · 2 of 2_; _Start at Day_ on the Film page). Look's main button is _Continue to Day_ (with the look's clothing) and _Try clothes on first_ opens Outfit.
- **A look no longer flattens the Day.** Continuing from Look stamped the look's mood / lighting / palette text over every slot's beat and its location over every Setting — a Day of one sentence in one place (and the compact prompts never used those notes). Now only the look's clothing carries over; the slots keep their own beats and places.
- **Fewer skin specks on Edit 2511 stills.** The freckle-like dark dots on legs and arms dropped by 21% on everyday poses and 43% on hard ones (arms overhead, sitting on the floor, lying, bending, leaning) with the `euler_ancestral` sampler on Edit 2511 Lightning 8. Poses held in 15 of 16 replays and the Cast's likeness was even or closer. Fewer specks, not none.
- **Two women on a lap, two men on the floor.** Two women's lap sex now puts one on top of the other (3 of 3 replays; before, they sat side by side in the chair). Two men's oral on the floor is a side view with the receiver standing against the wall: contact in 3 of 3, and the words match what Rapid draws.
- **Film mode starts at Play.** In Film (the play loop: Cast, Look, Outfit, Day, Story, Gallery, the default for a new install), opening the app's front page now goes to Play instead of the Generate tool. _All tools_ in Film's menu is now a real switch: it moves the workspace to Studio, with every tool, and opens it. Change back any time from Profile → Workspace. Settings already show only the essentials until _All settings…_.

## [v2.3.3] - 2026-10-07

- **Two men on Day, checked live for the first time.** Four problems in the text sent to ComfyUI, all fixed. (1) A man lead's couple stills opened with "SCENE: she is in … around her": the scene and outfit lines in front of the couple recipe now speak of him. (2) "In a silk robe over a slip" stayed on a man: the menswear swap now covers a slip, a camisole and panties (the verbs "slip off" and "slips into" are left alone). (3) "Sitting on his lap facing him… mid-sex" lost its compact recipe for two men and for two women. The same-sex rewording makes it "her partner's lap", which the layout reader didn't recognise. (4) Two men's oral drew no contact (0 of 3): it is now a side view with his boyfriend kneeling, and contact landed in 3 of 3. Rapid puts the lead on the receiving end however the beat reads (9 of 9), so the words say that. Two women keep their roles, which tested clean; changing them made renders worse. The two-men lap is now seated ("his boyfriend sits on the armchair"), not laid on its back. Live (Rapid, Edit 2511): two distinct men with their own faces in every render, clothed and adult.
- **Lying face to face stays lying.** A couple "lying face to face on the bed" came back seated (with or without a pose drawing). The couple recipe now says "Both lie on their sides on the bed, facing each other" first: both lying in 3 of 3 renders, against about 1 of 3, and the same for two men, two women and a man lead.
- **Suggestive pose drawings no longer contradict the beat.** Vacation and Suggestive draw clothed stills upright: a kneel or a lie is redrawn as a seated pair and a hug as a lean, to keep clothed stills away from sex layouts. Beats written lying, kneeling, on a lap or carried then got a drawing that said something else: "lying face to face on the bed" went out as a standing hands-on-hips pair, and "he kneels in front of her, kisses her knee" as two people sitting, which Edit 2511 followed. An audit of every beat found 10 such Suggestive beats (5 duo, 5 solo), and none in Vacation or Everyday. When the clothed drawing contradicts the beat, Day now sends no drawing and the words set the pose. In replays the kneel came out right in 3 of 3 without the drawing and in none with it. A pose you pick yourself is always drawn.
- **No third leg on solo stills lying back.** The stance words for a solo adult still on her back ("knees bent and thighs spread wide") described a different pose from the Image 3 guide, which folds one thigh toward the chest and puts the other knee out. Rapid drew both: two spread legs plus the guide's raised leg, on 5 of 5 renders of one beat. The line now says what the guide draws ("one knee drawn up toward her chest and the other leg bent out to the side — two legs only"): 0 of 4 on the same seeds. Leaving the guide out instead still drew the extra leg about half the time.
- **Quality says where it comes from.** Quality is layered — a tool's own choice beats the global one, and Fast runs as Good whenever a model is selected — but the chips didn't say which was in charge. Each tool's Engine panel now has a line under the Quality chips: _Set for Generate — other tools keep their own_, or _From Settings → Prompt quality (no choice saved for …)_, plus _Fast runs as Good…_ / _Custom uses the sampler and size defaults…_ where they apply (hidden where the tool's Quality preset owns the choice, as on Day and Outfit). When tools differ, Settings → Prompt quality lists each tool under its chip (Good / Best / Custom) instead of only saying that they differ.
- **Settings, simpler.** _System workflows_ is on for a fresh install (Heal & ready, the first-queue setup and a settings repair all turned it on anyway), with the Good ("final") quality Heal set alongside it — one setup step fewer; an existing choice is kept, and a fresh browser no longer pins the default before your saved settings arrive from the server. Prompt quality says that the sampler preset and size defaults apply to the Prompt tool, Outfit and batch queues — Day and Story pick canvas and steps per engine (a Day queued at _Max quality_ / _max_ size still rendered 8 steps at 1104×1472). **Removed:** _Also lock the pose with ControlNet_ — every ControlNet pose A/B lost to the OpenPose guide in words and Image 3 (Union, AnyPose, InstantX at 0.6), so the switch, its status line and the Day / Story ControlNet attach are gone (ControlNet itself stays everywhere else); and the _Legacy capsules_ pose-guide choice and its _Compare_ link — OpenPose (or OpenPose + hands) is what Day and Story use, and a stored legacy choice now reads as OpenPose.
- **Setup: the check packs say what is actually wrong, the check models download during setup, and LM Studio gets a vision model from the app.** The Play checks list (Settings → ComfyUI → Heal & ready) now tells "not installed" from "installed but not loading" by asking ComfyUI-Manager what is installed: ComfyUI_FaceAnalysis without its InsightFace Python package — the usual snag — shows the `pip install insightface onnxruntime` command for the Python ComfyUI runs on (portable Windows, a venv, Linux, each with what else it may need) with a Copy button; controlnet_aux that did not load points at the ComfyUI log. **Prepare checks** runs the face and pose checks once on a bundled sample picture (a clothed adult, rendered by the app) so InsightFace's and DWPose's first-use downloads happen during setup rather than in the first check of a Day (live: face 0.9 s, pose 3.4 s on a ready machine). With LM Studio as the LLM server and no vision model, the still-review row offers to download NSFWVision Qwen3-VL 8B (what the checks were tuned on) or Qwen3-VL 8B Instruct at Q4_K_M (~6 GB with the vision projector) through LM Studio's REST API, with progress; only these two can be requested.
- **Live progress and previews come back for the still that is rendering.** The page keeps at most three live streams open, and handed them out in the order stills were queued — but ComfyUI runs jobs by queue number (model-aware ordering, checks at the front), so with a whole Day queued at once (now that the reference checks answer instantly) the running still was often the one without a stream: no step progress, no preview. Streams now go to the running job and the next in line; a job further back gives its slot up and gets it back when its turn comes (it receives nothing until it runs). The cap stays at three.
- **Day: a friend is not a date.** Two-person Day stills on Edit 2511 (and Rapid's couple recipe) went out with the Suggestive couple's opening line — "Suggestive photo: A woman and a man together, both fully clothed, affectionate." — whenever the beat named a friend ("clinking glasses with a friend at the wine bar"); non-friend beats already got the neutral Day line. Every Day pair now opens "Day photo: … both fully clothed, both fully in frame.", friends keep their friend wording. Replayed live on three of the user's 2511 friend stills, two seeds each: the old wording leaned them in like a couple (face to face, head to head), the new one kept an arm's-length toast and a shared laptop, with faces, outfit and shoes unchanged.
- **Retired: the old `PromptTools*` ComfyUI nodes (`comfyui/`).** The 19 nodes that called the app's HTTP API from inside a ComfyUI graph (generate, format, character, duo, batch, lint, queue, job status, …) were still branded qwen-image-prompt, unchanged since August, offered the early SD-family model list, and no saved workflow used them. They are gone from the repository and the docs; the last version is in git history (`git show adb8b45a:comfyui/comfyui_image_prompt_tools/README.md`). The HTTP API they called is unchanged. Castcut nodes (`comfyui-nodes/castcut`) is the ComfyUI pack the app uses.
- **Castcut nodes 1.4.0: two-person pose reads off the queue, graph reads without the picture, and usage counts.** The read that keeps a couple in contact as two bodies (person masks → each person alone on grey → DWPose) runs in-process with the Impact Pack's own nodes, the YOLO model pinned to the CPU and DWPose on the CPU. Live against the queued graph on 7 of the user's stills: the same people in every read, keypoints within a median 0 px (worst 5.9 px on a ~1,400 px still, where the CPU mask edge differs), limb angles within a median 0° and at most 2.25° — well inside the pose check's 10–35° margins. Fix area, Face finish and the LoRA check read a still's graph from its PNG text through `/castcut/png-text` (5.6 KB instead of the 1.6 MB file). `/castcut/health` now counts what each route answered since ComfyUI started (served, failed, average time) and the app counts each time it had to fall back to the queue; the Castcut nodes card shows both, so a normal Day shows whether the checks really skip the queue.
- **Castcut nodes 1.3.0: pose checks and the Face finish probe skip the queue; unused uploads can be deleted.** The pose read behind Auto-review and the pose checks now runs DWPose inside ComfyUI on the CPU (comfyui_controlnet_aux's own detector, built once with CPU sessions, so a render keeps its GPU memory): live on 5 of the user's stills the keypoints were identical to the queued GPU node's (0.000 px), 0.4–0.7 s a still, without waiting for the render in progress. Face finish's "which face is hers" probe runs with the face checks (offline on 6 duo stills: the graph's positions and distances exactly). Settings → ComfyUI → Input folder offers _Delete N files_ after a scan when the pack can: it asks first (and warns when server storage is off), scans again, deletes only what that scan offers, and the pack itself keeps anything younger than a day, named by a running or pending job, or outside the folder's top level (checked live). The user's folder held 13,220 files (11 GB); the input list is 5.1 of the 8.2 MB `object_info`. `/castcut/health` gives the Castcut nodes card the queue counts in one small call and a line with what is checked without the queue and the free GPU memory. Older packs keep the queued graphs and the command.
- **Castcut nodes 1.2.0: face checks no longer wait for the render in progress.** The pack adds HTTP routes to ComfyUI: `/castcut/analyze` answers the face match (identity score, partner and face-lock checks) and _find the face_ on a plate directly — InsightFace buffalo_l kept loaded, on the CPU so it doesn't compete with the render — where they used to be queued as small graphs that waited behind the current render (45–95 s on Edit 2511) for ~0.5 s of work. Same numbers as the graphs (12 of the user's stills: distances within 7×10⁻⁸, identical boxes); live, a reference + 2 stills in 1.1 s, a plate's face in 0.4 s. `/castcut/stage` copies a checked still into ComfyUI's input folder itself (no download and upload; same content name as the app's uploads), and `/castcut/object-info-fingerprint` lets the app skip refetching the ~8 MB `object_info` while nodes and model files are unchanged. Without 1.2.0, or when a route fails, everything runs through queued graphs as before; Settings → ComfyUI → Castcut nodes shows an older pack as _Outdated_. Any ComfyUI with the jobs API: a waiting still's queue position comes from the jobs list (~1 KB) instead of the whole queue (35 KB with five stills queued), and the dress-plate check asks with HEAD instead of downloading the picture.
- **Day: a beat's own furniture no longer lands in the wrong place.** A yoga studio drew a bed ("sitting on the edge of the bed lacing boots") and a kitchen sat her on the open fridge door ("sitting on the rooftop ledge … city lights behind"): the beat/setting check did not read "the edge (foot, side) of the bed" as a bed, and had no ledge at all. Both are read now — a window ledge as a sill, any other ledge as an outdoor edge (rooftop, balcony, pool, street, …) — so the planner pairs them with a fitting place, and at queue time an already-planned beat is refitted ("sitting on the studio floor lacing boots", "on a stool at the counter") or drawn again.
- **Sport: no more white dot spray.** "An empty training hall with dust motes in sunbeams" drew the motes as white dots over the still; it is now "warm sunbeams across the floor", and "chalk dust in the air" / "dust hanging in the air" (climbing, gravel) are reworded the same way. Mud, ice and water spray are left as action.
- **Rapid: couple stills with no partner picked use the short couple recipe.** People → Duo with no Cast partner (or a failed stand-in) still sent the ~4–6k-character brief on Rapid AIO (and Qwen-Image 2.1, which hands clothed duos to Rapid); every other duo already used a recipe. A beat that names her partner (Vacation, Date night, Lazy Sunday, …) now gets the ~0.6–1k compact couple recipe. Live on Rapid v23, same seeds, 18 couple stills per arm: the man dressed as written 18/18 (brief 12/18 — shirtless otherwise), the beat done instead of both posed to camera (head on his shoulder 4/4 vs 0/4), her outfit 17/18 vs 18/18. Friend beats stay on the brief: with no partner the recipe turns the friend into "a man … affectionate" (10/10).
- **Picked shoes on two-person stills.** On Edit 2511 a duo still takes its shoes only from the dressed plate (Image 2 is the partner's face), so a plate in the wrong shoes put every duo still in them: picked light blue cork wedges came out as black flat sandals in 16 of 17 duo stills while solo stills, which also get the shoe picture, were right 26 of 27. The shoe check had read the plate correctly ("black strappy flat sandals") but wedges and platforms set no heel, so flats passed. Wedges and platforms now expect a raised heel and must be seen as wedge / platform / cork / espadrille; the feet pass describes solid wedge soles, not a heel post. Stored plates carry the version of the check that passed them, so plates passed by the old rules are checked once more on next use. Live: the feet pass on the user's plate gave wedges 4/4; eight of the user's duo stills replayed at their seeds with the fixed plate wore the wedges 7/8 (0/8 before), partner's own shoes kept 8/8.
- **Desktop: the AppImage, really.** v2.3.2 still failed: Next carries its own nested copy of sharp (`next/node_modules/@img/sharp-linuxmusl-x64`), which the 2.3.1 fix didn't reach. Staging now drops every musl-only `@img` package at any depth.

## [v2.3.2] - 2026-10-06

- **Fix an area, round two: no take is ever lost, you can see the change, and a one-click face fix.** The takes of a Fix are now tracked by a small store outside the dialog (`fix-area-session.ts`), polled on one timer whatever the dialog does — opening, closing, a remount by the development server's double mount, the lightbox re-rendering or a Day card refresh cannot cancel or drop a take; a Fix queued by a dialog that has already gone is tracked too. **Close the dialog while they render** and the still's card (Day, Story) and the lightbox button row show _Fixing… 1 of 2_; when both land the chip turns _Fix ready — compare_ and a toast says so — either reopens the results, _Use this_ works as before. _Keep original_ still drops unstarted jobs; closing results you have seen to the end keeps nothing behind. The 2.3.1 fixes stay: a finished take's ComfyUI record is kept, the adult check gives up after a minute with _Check again_ (the withheld take keeps its picture), waits count seconds. Checked on a StrictMode `next dev` server against the stubbed ComfyUI (the e2e, which also counts cancel calls: 0) and live against the real one (Rapid AIO, 2 stills, 4 takes: both shown in the dialog; close-while-rendering → chip → _Fix ready — compare_ → used; no cancels, no console errors), and the lifecycle under open → closed → open is unit-tested. **Seeing the change:** _Zoom to the area_ crops the original and every take to the painted box plus a margin at one scale, and a _Before / After_ wipe under the grid drags the selected take over the original (click a take to put it in the wipe); the painted-area switch stays. **Fix the face:** one button finds the face (the app's InsightFace locate, the largest face), paints it and runs the fix with the Cast's face as the identity reference where the engine reads pictures (Image 2 of the positive encoder — the face picture the still's own graph used, checked to show a face, else a face cut from its plate; one older face crop held only hair). A/B on four clothed solo stills (Rapid AIO ×2, Edit 2511, Qwen-Image 2.1; InsightFace distance to the Cast face, lower = closer; original 0.54 / 0.71 / 0.22 / 0.13): a grey-fill redraw at full denoise with no reference 0.84 / 0.99 / 0.72 / 0.81 and with the reference 0.85 / 0.82 / 0.78 / 0.21 — the head turned or another person; **the still itself as Image 1 at denoise 0.5 with the reference 0.53 / 0.74 / 0.26 / 0.15** keeps the pose and the Cast (4 of 4), so that is how _Fix the face_ runs (a painted area keeps the grey fill at 1.0). **Day:** _Undo the fix_ now walks back through every fix made on a slot (_Undo the fix (2 more)_); _Keep the fix_ clears the stack; the Gallery keeps every version anyway. **Phone Story:** a finished beat's ⋯ menu has _Fix an area…_. **Narrower blend:** the soft edge where a fix meets the original is half as wide (about 0.6% of the long side instead of 1.2%; the regenerated region is unchanged). A/B on the first round's four stills by compositing one raw sample per still offline through each ramp (pixel-exact, no extra renders): pixels outside the hard mask identical for every variant; the narrow ramp made fewer new edges on 3 of 4 (0.70 → 0.40, 1.17 → 0.64, 0.44 → 0.29; the knit-over-skin still 1.57 → 2.01 but its band is half as tall) and kept more of the original texture in the band on 4 of 4 (0.85 → 0.93, 0.81 → 0.89, 0.61 → 0.82, 1.07 → 1.06), no seam at 4–5×; a contrast-following feather snapped single pixels and speckled two of four, not shipped. The soft band on the knit-over-skin still is the sample drawing knit lower than the original, not the feather — it is shorter now, not gone. Renders: 4 raw samples, 12 face A/B, 4 live through the UI.
- **Two takes: the likelier-good take is shown first.** With _Two takes, you pick_ on, once both takes of an intimate still land the app counts faces, hands, bodies, wrists and ankles on each (one small ComfyUI check per take: the Impact Pack's face and hand models, DWPose, and the nsfw part models when installed) and puts the take with fewer counted oddities first on the card, with a note saying what the other counted ("Take 2: Shown first — the other take counted one face. A hint only: you pick."). Labels and _Keep this one_ stay with their takes. Nothing is redone on it, and it is only a hint: measured on 343 of the user's own intimate two-person stills (kept vs. deleted or re-rendered) and the 60 judged by eye in the limb study, no count, person-mask, skin-region or per-person pose signal — nor any combination, hand-built or learned — came near the bar for an automatic redo (60% of bad stills caught at 10% false alarms): the best caught 17–33% at 10%, and the oddity count orders a bad/good pair right 35–47% of the time, ties 28–44%, wrong 20–25%. So two-person intimate stills still skip _Redo pose misses_, Auto-review's pose check and _Best of two_. The count runs once per pair, only while the Day queue is idle, and switches itself off for the session when ComfyUI lacks a node or model (the status line says which).
- **Reference checks: a wrong picture is never sent silently.** Two of this week's bugs were "wrong picture" bugs — the prompt and the graph looked right, only the pixels were wrong (the Day partner's face upload was an unrelated whole-body picture for four days; a look's face lock was an old underwear plate that nude stills used whole as Image 1). Every picture a Day, Story or Outfit still goes out with is now checked against its role before it is queued, with one InsightFace bounding-box run per picture (cached by the uploaded file, so a Day's face crop is measured once — under a second after the first): a face reference (face crop, face lock, face-break crop, `day-nude-face-*`) must show exactly one face that fills a fair share of the picture (not a whole-body plate — live, the user's face crop reads 43% of the height, their Cast plates 10–11%, the unrelated partner picture 8%); a Cast partner's face must also match the partner's own plate and not the lead's (the app's face-recognition distance); the clothing image must not be a face crop or a `cast-plate-…` file; a `cast-plate-…` file is never accepted as a face, before any pixels are read. On a clear miss the safe path is taken where there is one and the card says so: a face lock that fails is left out and the face is cropped from the look's plate; a partner face that fails leaves the partner invented ("Tomas's face picture doesn't show a face — check Cast → Tomas. This still invents the partner instead."); a clothing miss is said on the card / in the tray and the still still goes out. A check that errors, times out or has no ComfyUI never blocks a queue (it logs and passes). Settings → ComfyUI → Workflow library health gains _Reference pictures_: the active Cast's plate and face lock, the Day partner's plate and face lock (compared with their plate and the lead's) and the invented partner's face, each OK / what is wrong / not checked. Day's Partner tile shows the face picture actually sent once a two-person still went out. Live on the user's own input files (read-only): `day-nude-face-…` OK; `day-partner-vl-…` (the fox-girl picture) fails as a partner face; `cast-plate-base-…` and `cast-plate-prepared-…` fail as faces (by name, and by pixels: one face a tenth of the height).
- **Fix an area: rendered takes are never lost.** A finished take's ComfyUI record was deleted the first time it was read, so one lost status reply left it "missing" though it had rendered; the record now stays. The adult check on a take now gives up after a minute (it waited forever on a busy LM Studio) with a _Check again_ button, and waiting takes count the seconds.
- **Desktop: the Linux AppImage builds again.** v2.3.1's AppImage failed to bundle (sharp's musl/Alpine build was traced into the desktop server and linuxdeploy can't resolve musl libc); staging now drops `@img/*linuxmusl*`. The 2.3.1 `.deb`, `.dmg`, `.exe` and Docker image were unaffected.
- **Pose editor: the picture behind the figure, and a head that turns three-quarters.** _Show her picture_ now has a _Strength_ slider (10–90%, faint by default, remembered with the toggle), so the Cast's plate can be read against the figure without hiding it; the picture is fitted whole into the still's canvas, as before. The _Head_ row is now _Left · ¾ left · Front · ¾ right · Right · Up · Down_: the three-quarter chips turn the head half-way (both eyes drawn, the nose off the middle, only the seen cheek's ear), the chip that matches the figure lights, and they are greyed on a figure seen from the side as the full turns are. Every head chip reaches the words under the figure and so the still's prompt — _head turned to her left, face in profile toward the right of the picture, looking away from the camera_; _head turned three-quarters to her right, face angled toward the left of the picture, eyes off the camera_; _chin up_; _head bowed_ — and a pose picked from Day's list or the real poses keeps its own words and gains the head (_waving: one arm raised high, head turned three-quarters to her left, …_), said once however many chips are tapped. The long form is what it takes: on Edit 2511 (Day's clothed engine, face-locked to Image 1) _head turned to her left_ alone left her facing the camera 4/4 on the wave beat; the full profile line turned it 4/4 (at the end of the Pose sentence or leading it, 2 seeds each). Known limit: the three-quarter line did not turn her on 2511 in 2 tries — she faced the camera as the map draws her — so expect _¾ left / ¾ right_ to read as a hint there; Rapid AIO and Qwen-Image 2.1 are untested. The dashed ghost of the pose you started from and _Reset_ (back to it, undo-able) are unchanged; arrow keys still nudge the focused joint and the slider works by keyboard.
- **Cold loads that read before storage was ready.** The _Tomorrow's Day_ habit nudge and the phone shell's Story tab read the Play metrics once on mount; when IndexedDB had not hydrated yet they stayed empty until the window was focused again (about one load in six on a busy machine). Both now read again as soon as storage is ready. A page that loads while the server is rate-limiting now waits the server's _Retry-After_ (up to 10 s) between session retries and keeps asking in the background (every 15 s, for two minutes), so the navigation fills in without a reload.
- **E2E suite: the known flakes.** The three "fails only under full-suite parallelism" tests were the rate limiter (every Playwright worker is one client); the config lifts it for its own server, and `e2e/global-setup.ts` now refuses a multi-worker run against a hand-started server that still has the default limit, with the fix in the message (`API_RATE_LIMIT_MAX=100000`, `--workers=1`, or `PROMPT_E2E_ALLOW_RATE_LIMIT=1`). The two tests that still failed under that limit were seed races, not timing: Story's still/clip toggle seeded the Cast through localStorage alone (lost to the empty IndexedDB copy on hydrate) and the habit nudge wrote its metrics from an init script's own asynchronous IndexedDB transaction (lost to the app's hydrate); both seed through `seedSettingsCacheOnNextLoad` / `putAppKv` now, as do four more specs that used the same raw seed. `putAppKv` itself opened IndexedDB without a version and skipped a missing `kv` store, so a seed written before the app page had finished creating the database was silently dropped (8 of 12 parallel repeats of the habit-nudge test); it now opens at the app's own schema and creates the stores. The command-palette smoke test typed blind before the palette's search box had focus (1 in 11 runs) and now fills the box. The Outfit pose-editor spec covers the picture strength slider, the three-quarter chips, the words line and Reset.
- **Fix an area: the takes show up, and you can see where they changed.** In the development server (React's double mount) the dialog thought it was closed as soon as it opened, so pressing Fix cancelled both takes — the button spun and nothing came back, though the take ComfyUI had started still rendered. (The release build was not affected.) The results now also lighten the painted area on the original and each take, with a switch to hide it.
- **Fix: beige bra and briefs back in sex scenes.** A look whose plate had changed kept its old base-layer plate (`cast-plate-…`, a whole body in beige underwear) as its face lock, and nude Day / Story stills used that "face" whole as Image 1. A face lock that is a Cast plate no longer counts as a face picture: nude stills crop the face from the look's plate, as without a lock.

## [v2.3.1] - 2026-10-05

- **Fix an area — paint over what's wrong, only that part is redrawn.** In the picture lightbox (Gallery, Day, Story, Outfit) _Fix an area_ opens a brush over the still: paint or erase with mouse, pen or touch, a brush-size slider, Clear (keys on the picture: `[` / `]` size, `E` paint / erase, Delete clears), and an optional _What should be there?_ box. Day and Story cards have it in their ⋯ menu too (_Fix an area…_). _Fix_ renders two takes on the still's **own engine** — its graph as ComfyUI saved it (model, LoRAs, prompt), queued back to back at the front — with only the painted area re-sampled: Image 1 is the still with that area filled flat grey (the plate and pose guide are dropped), a hard noise mask on the painted area grown past the blend, full denoise, and the still's prompt plus "Fix only the marked area: <your words, or _clean, natural anatomy, nothing extra_>". Each take is composited back through a feathered mask, so **every pixel outside the brush (and its feather, about 1.2% of the long side) is byte-identical** to the original. The result shows the original and both takes side by side: _Use this_ or _Keep original_ (unstarted jobs are dropped from the queue). A used fix is a new Gallery entry under the original (_Fixed area_, with the before/after wipe); on Day the slot shows the fix and the slot sheet keeps the picture before it (_Undo the fix_), on Story it becomes the beat's shown take (the take arrows go back), on Outfit it replaces the try-on card. Takes of an adult still pass the adult-appearance gate before they are shown. Live A/B on four of the user's clothed stills (Rapid AIO ×2 with a stray hand / stray fingers, Edit 2511 with garbled lettering on a sweater, Qwen-Image 2.1 with a painted red blob), outside-the-feather diff 0 on every render: grey fill + full denoise cleaned 4/4; grey at 0.75 left a grey patch 4/4 (so the grey fill always runs at 1.0); the still itself as Image 1 re-painted the stray hand on Rapid (as in the limb study) and kept the stray fingers, tied on Edit 2511 / 2.1. Through the app (lightbox → brush → _Fix_ → _Use this_, two takes each): 8 of 8 takes cleaned — stray hand 2/2, stray fingers 2/2, lettering 2/2, blob 2/2 — and every take byte-identical outside the soft mask the graph composited with; seams invisible at 2×. Limits: a large area painted across two different materials (knit over skin on the 2511 sweater) leaves a soft band where the feather blends them; it is a regional pass, so fused or phantom limbs on two-person stills are redrawn from context (the limb study's finding) — reroll those. Found and fixed while testing live: overlapping status polls could mark a landed take as lost (the server drops a job's history once read). Renders: 12 A/B + 10 through the UI.
- **Fix: a Cast partner on Day never appeared.** Their face was loaded by file name, and the loader tried the global identity-lock picture first — so every two-person still since the partner face moved to its own upload was sent that picture (on the user's install, an unrelated character) and the engine invented a stranger. A named file now always loads itself; the identity picture is only the fallback when no file is named (Roleplay's lead photo).
- **Fix: "Pick the best engine per pose" no longer moves stills onto Qwen-Image 2.1.** Its "solid" scores rest on 2–3 sweep stills, so a few of your redos on Rapid were enough to send a still there — a Late Night still came back distorted with "it holds this pose better". Hand-offs now go only between Rapid AIO and Edit 2511; picking Qwen-Image 2.1 as the Day engine is unchanged.
- **Bald is a hair colour choice** on the Cast's Appearance: it says "a bald head" in every picture's description, and greys out hair length and style (Hair length → Bald / shaved still gives a shaved head).
- **Scissors renders as the missionary pose**, like 69 and face-sit render as seated oral: Rapid can't draw scissoring (stills kept coming out odd). Couples get the missionary recipe (8/8), two women their side-by-side version (4/4), two men keep their kneeling recipe; the map and the clip follow, and "scissoring" is dropped from the still's and clip's words.
- **Five more adult pose maps match their words:** lap (he sits, she straddles facing him), wall (face to face with a knee raised — or from behind, hands braced, when the beat or a window says so), standing (she leans forward braced, partner behind), kneeling (face to face) and all fours on the bed (hands and knees, partner kneeling behind).
- **Intimate clips animate the pose the still shows.** Face-sit and 69 stills are drawn as seated oral, so their clips now animate oral (and the scene line no longer asks for a 69); scissors stays seated; a wall still is face to face unless the beat or a window says from behind; two women keep the hand motion their stills draw; two men's scissors stays kneeling.
- **Scissors pose map matches its words.** The recipe seats them ("sit facing each other, each leaning back on their hands, legs scissored together") but the map drew two lying bodies pulled hip to hip; it now draws both sitting, leaning back on straight arms, knees interlocked.
- **Day: the pose fits the place — no bed on a street.** Everyday and Suggestive draw the beat and the Setting separately, and the beat names its own furniture: "lying on her side on the sofa, reading" went out with a busy plaza (the still put a sofa on the plaza). A new pure helper (`scene-surface.ts`) reads the kind of place from the Setting and names what is there to lie, sit, kneel and lean on (park → the grass, beach → a towel on the sand, plaza → the fountain's edge or a bench, street → a low step and nowhere to lie, bedroom → the bed, living room → the sofa, kitchen → a stool at the counter). The planner now draws beats and settings that fit each other; at Queue a clothed still's beat has its furniture moved to the place (a lying beat where nobody lies becomes a sit there), the stance lines ("LYING = … ON the bed/couch/floor"), the slot's baseline pose, the recipe's kneel and the layout cue's "on the floor" follow the Setting, and a Day-drawn beat the place can't host at all (cooking at the stove on a street) is drawn again. Pose packs skip lying poses on slots that can't host them. The queue-time prompt check gains _scene-surface-mismatch_. Over every beat × setting pair the planner could draw (30k finished prompts on Rapid / Edit 2511 / Qwen-Image 2.1): 4,411 stills with a furniture/setting contradiction before, 0 after. Live A/B on four beats that contradicted (Rapid AIO and Edit 2511, two seeds, same graphs): the sofa on the plaza 4/4 → she sits on the fountain's edge 4/4; a lawn beat on subway stairs rendered a park 4/4 → the stairs 4/4; a rug on the sidewalk 3/4 → 0/4 (kneel held 8/8); a bed beat on a night street drew no bed either way. Adult moods are unchanged.
- **Kept seeds for intimate stills.** When you keep an Intimate / Raunchy Day still (favorite or 4★+ in the Gallery, or the take you pick from two), its seed is remembered for that pose layout on that engine — a small rolling list (six, newest first) per layout × engine. Later intimate stills of that layout on that engine start from those seeds in turn, then one random roll so new keepers can turn up, then round again; a _Looks wrong_ redo, a same-seed redo and a pair's second take always roll a new seed, a requeue never repeats the seed it replaces, and a still on a remembered seed that you mark _Looks wrong_ or delete forgets that seed. Local to this install: the seeds sync with the pose outcome stats through the server (merged per browser, a stale copy never rolls them back); the rotation stays in the browser.
- **Looks wrong, in each Day card's ⋯ menu.** One click on a landed still (any mood; most useful for Intimate stills, where merged bodies and mixed-up layouts are seed luck) queues that slot again on a new seed with the same words, and the card says _Redone — looked wrong_. The verdict counts as a bad take for that pose layout × engine in the pose outcome stats — as strong as a Gallery delete, and a redo never softens it — so the learned weak-pose hint, the pose-pack skip and _Pick the best engine per pose_ see it. On an unpicked pair of two takes both takes count.
- **Two takes for intimate stills, you pick.** A switch under Day → Advanced (shown on an Intimate / Raunchy Day; off by default, not part of the Quality preset) queues each intimate still twice back to back — the same words, two seeds, so the model-aware queue keeps them together. When both land the card shows them side by side with _Keep this one_ under each; the other take stays beside the pick in the slot sheet (_Use the other take_ swaps them, any time). A take that fails or that the adult check withholds leaves the other on its own. These stills are never paired by pose score (Best of two) or redone by the pose / Auto-review checks: automatic defect checks on two-person stills were unreliable, so the player judges. The pick counts as kept / replaced in the pose × engine stats.
- **Two-person intimate stills are no longer redone automatically for the pose.** Redo pose misses and Auto-review skip the pose check on them: the engines take those poses from the words, and the check can't judge them (DWPose merges the two bodies about 40% of the time), so a redrawn adult guide only raised false redos. Use _Looks wrong_ / _Two takes_ for those.
- **Clothed one-person clips render on WAN** even with LTX-2.5 picked: LTX-2.5 lost the face (mean match 0.33 vs WAN's 0.58 over 8 Day stills). A switch under Animate keeps LTX-2.5 for them (faster; the face drifts). Two-person adult clips stay on WAN as before; adult one-person clips keep the picked engine.
- **Pose report card refreshed for 2.3 — no pose got worse; Suggestive beats no longer rerolled at Queue.** Every one-person Day pose (72 layouts, 13 Suggestive and 17 Vacation beats) re-run on Rapid AIO, Edit 2511 and Qwen-Image 2.1 Pruna with the app's own graphs (isolated app + capture proxy, demo Cast), judged by eye; 2.3 changed only the Suggestive words, so those rows count only 2.3 renders and the others add the new renders to the previous tallies (unchanged graphs are the same picture at the same seed — checked to the pixel — and were reused, not rendered again). On 2.3 code: Postures + Everyday 43/43 Rapid, 45/45 Edit 2511, 38/48 Qwen-Image 2.1; Sport 28/36, 29/34, 27/43; Suggestive 26/26, 26/27, 24/26; Vacation 28/29, 35/35, 25/26 — in line with the previous card, the four cells it held that missed once each have their other seeds right; Rapid _jump_ and _pockets_ (1/3 → 2/2) and Suggestive _lying on her side_ on Rapid and Edit 2511 got better. **Fix:** Queue's mood check only vouched for a Suggestive beat outside its own daypart's pool by cue words ("lingerie", "robe loosely" …) that the 2.3 clothed rewrite removed, so the windowsill sit, the patio dance and the stomach-lying beats were swapped for other beats when they sat in another slot (3 of 13); any built-in solo Suggestive beat now fits any slot (couple beats still need companions) — 13 of 13 reach their stills, the three right 18 of 18. Picked shoes (black pumps, 8 poses × 3 engines): worn 23 of 24; the dress plates drifted the outfit on 2.1 (white trousers) and 2511 (a grey-green tracksuit) — one plate each, noted as a lead. Card moves: Rapid no longer hands off or skips _jump_, Edit 2511's _jump shot_ no longer moves. Tried and not shipped: Rapid's boxing cue on Qwen-Image 2.1 (3/4 with it vs 2/3 without). Renders: 317 stills + 4 dress plates. See `docs/pose-report-card.md`.
- **Cast face crop finds the face, on any plate.** The face crop Day and Story use as the identity image (face-break and nude Image 1, the Klein face reference) and as Face finish's reference was cut from the top of the plate, so on a plate where she lies down it held only hair (the demo Nora lying plate) — and Face finish, conditioned on that, moved faces further from her. The crop is now a padded square (2.2× the face, nudged up for the hair) around the face InsightFace finds on the plate (`/api/face-locate`: the FaceAnalysis `FaceBoundingBox` in a check-priority ComfyUI graph, the plate staged once by content and the answer cached per plate; a face it misses is looked for again in the plate turned a quarter each way). Upright plates crop as before but tighter (head and collar, less outfit). When no face is found the old top window is kept and Day says "No face found on this plate — Prepare plate or use an upright photo" (with the Cast link), and Face finish skips the pass instead of conditioning it on hair; with no detector (ComfyUI off, no FaceAnalysis pack) nothing changes. Live A/B on the lying plate (Rapid AIO Day face-break graphs, stand + walk beats × 2 seeds, face distance to the plate): old crop 0.737 → new 0.667, closer 3/4 (walk 0.844 → 0.652, 0.557 → 0.427; the backlit stand beat about even), poses and outfit unchanged.
- **Fix: one Cast's Story scenes landed in another Cast's session.** On the demo install Tomas's Story held Nora's two scenes (same ids and times) under Tomas's bible and settings. Reproduced: Nora's still was rendering, Tomas was made the active Cast on another page, and opening Story bound Tomas — but the page's reel ref still held Nora's scenes until the next render, and the Gallery sync patched her finished still into "the" story, which was now Tomas's. Story's writes now go through a per-session guard: a Cast switch points the reel at the new session at once, every scene is stamped with its Cast (`castId`) when written, a scene stamped for another Cast is never written into this session or saved in its library entry (also on load and sync merges; an imported Cast re-stamps its scenes), a reel tied to another Cast's session is always parked under that Cast, and scene cards or a bible written for the previous lead are dropped instead of being played under the new one. Unit tests plus an e2e of the reported path (`e2e/story-cast-bleed.spec.ts`, failed before the fix).
- **Day: piggyback and head on a shoulder are placed body by body.** On Edit 2511 and Rapid AIO two-person clothed poses come from the words, not the map: a piggyback came out as a front carry in his arms and a head on a shoulder as a cuddle with both heads tilted together (or his on hers), whatever map was drawn. The clothed two-person recipes (the Suggestive couple recipe and the compact everyday duo recipe) now say, after the Moment, "Pose: piggyback ride: she rides up on his back, her arms around his shoulders from behind and her legs wrapped around his waist; he stands leaning forward, his hands holding her thighs at his hips; both faces toward the camera, seen from the side" and "Pose: side by side, she leans her head down onto his shoulder, her cheek resting on his shoulder, eyes closed; he sits upright, head straight, his arm around her" (a same-sex partner by the name the recipe uses, "her friend" / "her girlfriend"; a man lead's couple recipe the other way round; "stands upright" on a standing beat). Replays of a clothed Day duo graph, same seeds and map, old words + the generic cue line vs new: Edit 2511 piggyback 0/4 → 2/4 (one more a shoulder ride), head on a shoulder 0/4 → 4/4; Rapid couple recipe head on a shoulder 1/4 → 4/4. Not on Rapid's long two-person brief, where the same words as the cue line changed nothing (piggyback 3/4 both — Rapid already carries on the back there — head on a shoulder 0/4 both). The hand-drawn head-on-shoulder references (all four) and the side-view piggyback are no longer drafts; the front and three-quarter piggybacks stay drafts. Renders: 40 replays.
- **Balanced Day: cheap checks first, no wasted face passes.** Timing real 4-slot Everyday Days showed where Balanced's extra minutes went: the pose check (DWPose) sat 25–250 s in ComfyUI's queue behind Face finish passes and renders (a `front` job runs newest-first, so every later front job jumped it), the plate cut-out waited up to 18 min and timed out, and Face finish (≈36–44 s per pass on Edit 2511) ran on takes the pose check then redid — 1 wasted pass per redo. Now: **quick checks run right after the current job** (DWPose, face probes and cut-outs get a ComfyUI queue number below every front job, `checkQueueNumber`); **on Balanced the pose / gesture / realism check runs on each still as it lands and Face finish waits for it** (`day-finish-order.ts`), so a redone take is never face-finished; **Face finish probes the face first** (a few seconds, before the pass is held for its model's turn) and skips a face already within 0.35 of the Cast face — scored on 97 of the user's past passes, the 10 one-person passes that started under 0.35 came out further 7 times — and skips when the Cast face crop shows no face at all; **a one-person pass is kept only when her face came out closer** (two people already were; 18 of 61 past one-person passes ended further away). Best (Auto-review) keeps its old order. Found while measuring: Day's face crop for a plate where she lies down is the top of the frame — hair, no face (the demo Cast "Nora"); Face finish conditioned on it moved 3 of 4 faces further from her (0.76 → 0.85), and is now skipped with a note to use an upright plate.
- **Story: the writer is waited for, not replaced by the built-in cards.** Four Stories played end to end (two Casts, a man and a woman lead, Clean and Suggestive, a built-in Part with the look described from the photo and a custom Part; 7 scenes each, Rapid AIO, LM Studio) rolled the generic built-in cards ("Hours later", "Uninvited guest", "Opposite play") on 2–4 of 7 rolls per Story: the server allows two chat calls at once, and a pick starts the still writer and the scene writer while the last still's checks run, so the roll failed _busy_ at once. Story's scene, bible and still writers now wait for a slot (up to 90 s), a transient LM Studio error ("400 failed to decode", 429, 5xx) is tried twice more (after 1.5 s and 5 s), and a fallback that still happens is logged. Replaying the two Stories with the most fallbacks: built-in rolls 3 of 7 → 0 of 8 and 2 of 7 → 1 of 8, and the stills that repeated the previous one's words (86–100% shared phrasing on 2–3 beats per Story) went to at most 58%. When the built-in cards do show: a card built on another card no longer reads "the opposite tactic of hours later" (it refers back to the last beat with a story of its own), openings name the lead instead of "you" or the custom Part's text, and for a man lead the quoted earlier beat is no longer flipped to "her hat".
- **Story: the outfit carries over.** The next still is told the last outfit any earlier still named when the previous one named none (a floral dress → sequins → a blazer in three beats), and to write its own action — two beats repeated the previous still word for word (86–100% shared phrasing). Without the writer, a still keeps the last outfit instead of "the beat outfit" and no longer restates the look over the reference. The everyday-clothes line no longer lands on a still that names a blazer, waistcoat, dress, shirt, jeans or skirt.
